iṉit tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vēṟāyatōr taṉi moḻiyāyiṉum aḵtu āriyampōl attuṇaip paḻamaiyuṭaiyataṉṟu, nirampavum putitāyt tōṉṟiya toṉṟām eṉṟuraippār urai poruntāmaiyum ammoḻi yārāycciyāṉ oru ciṟitu kāṭṭutum. tamiḻil oruporuḷai yuṇarttutaṟkup pala poruḷ niṟṟal piṅkalantai mutalāṉa nikaṇṭu nūlkaṟṟa ciṟu makārum naṉkuṇarvar. oru poruḷai yuṇarttutaṟku oru collē cālumākavum aṅṅaṉam pala coṟkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭuva teṉṉaiyeṉa nuṇukiyārāyiṉ ataṉuṇmai iṉitu pulappaṭā niṟkum. eṭuttukkāṭṭumiṭattuk, kuṇattiṉai yuṇartta uka coṟkaḷum, uyar viṉai yuṇartta kae coṟkaḷum, valiviṉai yuṇartta ṅaca coṟkaḷum, accattiṉai yuṇartta kakū coṟkaḷum, malaiyiṉai yuṇartta ua coṟkaḷum varukiṉṟaṉa. piṟavum ivvāṟē. orē kālattu ic coṟkaḷellām oruṅku piṟantaṉavākā. ovvōr collum ovvōr kālattut tōṉṟic cilakālamellām naṭaipeṟṟu accol vaḻaṅkiya makkaḷōṭu atuvumoḻiya, avarkkup piṉṉeḻum makkaḷōṭu piṟa putiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟi avaiyumavvāṟē naṭaipeṟṟoḻiya iṅṅaṉam moḻi nāṭōṟun tiripeyti varum iyaṟkaittāmeṉka. ivvāṟu coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyum vaḻaṅkiyum maṟaintum pōtarap pōtarum moḻi nākarika mutircciyuṭaiya makkaṭkuriyatāyiṉ ataṉkaṇ avvak kālattirunta aṟivuṭaiyōrāl eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷil accoṟkaḷ kaṉmēleḻuttuppōṟ cetukkappaṭṭu nilaipeṟum. ammoḻi aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi anākarikamuṭaiya makkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭuva toṉṟāyiṉ ataṉkaṭ kālantōṟum piṟanta coṟkaḷ ellām oruṅkē maṟaintoḻiya ammoḻiyum nāḷaṭaivil iṟantoḻiyumeṉka. oru kālattirunta makkaḷ oru poruṭkaṇ uḷḷa oru kuṇacciṟappup paṟṟi apporuṭkoru peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar. avvāṟu vaḻaṅki varukaiyil appeyar paṉmuṟaiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varutaliṉ ataṉ kāraṇam maṟaintatu; maṟaiyavē, accollaiyun tamakku vēṇṭiyavāṟellām tirivupaṭuttik koṇṭu varutaliṉ accol kaṭaiciyil vaḻakku vīḻntoḻiyap piṉ varuvōr ac collaiviṭṭu apporuṭkaṇ tām ciṟappāyk kaṇṭa kuṇampaṟṟi vēṟu peyar tarukuvar. eṭuttukkāṭṭuṅkāl, marakkompukaḷai veṭṭukiṉṟa karuvikkuk kōṭari eṉṟu peyar (kōṭu - kompu; ari - arivatu); ataṉaip paṉmuṟaiyālum vaḻaṅki varum makkaḷ ataṉ kāraṇampaṟṟi maṟantamaiyiṉ appeyaraik kōṭāli eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. iccol taṉ uṇmai vaṭivattuṭaṉ tamiḻ nūl kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭātoḻiyiṉ aḵtiññāṉ ṟiṟantu pōyirukku meṉpatiṟ ṟaṭaiyeṉṉai? muṉvaḻakku vīḻntoḻinta coṟkaḷun tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭiruttaliṉ piṉvaruvōr avaṟṟaiyum orō vorukā leṭuttu vaḻaṅkutaluñ ceytiṭukiṉṟār. aṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅkuvatuñ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṟkē payaṉ paṭuvataṉṟi, ellā makkaḷukkum uriya ulakavaḻakkil aḵtillāmaiyāl ulakavaḻakkiṟku yāṇṭum putuc coṟkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭu meṉṟal orutalaiyām. aṟṟēl, kālaṅkaṭōṟum paḻañcoṟkaḷ vīḻntum putuccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyum varumāyiṉ moḻiyē muṉnilai keṭṭup piṉ vēṟōrnilai eytumālō veṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu, oru moḻi mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṟuṅ kālattup piṟanta pattut tokutiyiṟ cērnta coṟkaḷ aṉaittum ellārāṉum nāṭōṟum tiriviṉṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭum iṉṟiyamaiyā neṟimaiyuṭaiya vākaliṉ accoṟkaṇ maṭṭum vaḻakku vīḻntaḻiyā veṉpatu naṭaimuṟaiyil vaittuk kaṇṭukoḷka. am mutaṟpiṟavic coṟkaḷpaṟṟiyē oru moḻi vaḻakku vīḻātu nilaipeṟumeṉ ṟuṇarntu koḷka. iṉi accoṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟum piṟantu vaḻaṅki maṟaiyap pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cellumeṉṟal paḻakkavaṟivuṭaiyā rellārkkum iṉitu pulaṉām. paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaḷ malaināṭukaḷiṉuṅ kaṭaṟkaraiyōraṅkaḷiṉum vāḻntavareṉpatu piṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭumātalāl, avar vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyil malai, kaṭal eṉṉumiraṇṭaiyuñ cuṭṭum peyarkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭum. accoṟkaḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum paḻaiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷil āyntu poṟukki, evveccol evvek kālattu nūliṟ ṟōṉṟiṟṟeṉṟu uṟutip paṭutti ammuṟaiyāl accoṟkaḷai muṉ piṉ vaittuk kaṇippiṉ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ kālam iṉitu nāṭṭappaṭum. iṅṅaṉaṅ kālaṅ kaṇikkum muṟaiyiṉ arumaiyai naṉkuṇarntaṉṟē māri eṉṉum āṅkilavāciriyar tāmeḻutiya āṅkilamoḻi varalāṟṟil itaṉaik kaṭaippiṭittu ataṉ kālavaḷavaic  cevvaṉē kuṟittiṭṭār. atupōlavē, tamiḻnūl valla āciriyarum immuṟai vaḻātu ārāyntu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ paḻañciṟappai niṟuvutaṟkup perituṅ kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟār. ikkaṭṭuraiyil emmāl aṅṅaṉaṅ kālauṟuti ceyvataṟkuk kūṭātāyiṉum. oru poruḷaic cuṭṭutaṟkup pala coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭum attuṇaiyē paṟṟit tamiḻmoḻi mikap paḻaiya moḻiyā meṉṟu kūṟutal kuṟṟamāmāṟillai yeṉka. atu kiṭakka.
iṉic, colvaḻakkāṉē yaṉṟic colluccarippu muṟaiyāṉun tamiḻ mikap paḻaiyatoṉṟātal kāṭṭappaṭum. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ellāc coṟkaḷ mutaliṉum ṭ, ṇ, r, l, ḻ, ḷ, ṟ, ṉ eṉṉum eṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ nillāveṉa ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil oru viti kāṇappaṭutal ciṟitilakkaṇa aṟivuṭaiyārum aṟivar. ṭamarukam, raku, rāmaṉ, lampakam mutaliyaṉavāka āriya moḻiccoṟkaḷ avveḻuttukkaḷai mutaṟkoṇṭu varumākavum, tamiḻ maṭṭum aṅṅaṉam avveḻuttukkaḷai mutaṟkaṇ niṟuttuk koḷḷāmaiyum, ap piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyun tāṉeṭuttu vāṅkum pōtu iṭamarukam, iraku, irāmaṉ, ilampakam eṉa uyir mutal niṟuttu vaḻaṅkutalum eṉṉai eṉṟu ārāykiṉṟuḻi ataṉiyalpu naṉkuṇarak kiṭakkum. paṭaippukkālat toṭakkattilē tōṉṟiya makkaḷiyalpum, nam paḻakkattiṟ kāṇum ciṟumakār iyaṟkaiyum tammuṭ perumpāṉmaiyuñ camamuṭaiyaṉavām. iḵtaṟivuṭaiyā rellārkkum oppa muṭinta vōr uṇmaiyēyākum. piḷḷaipparuvattilē, col uccarittaṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāk karuvikaḷāṉa itaḻ, nā, pal, aṇṇaṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyavāṟu iyaṅkāmaiyiṉ ap paruvattē ammakār vaḻaṅkuñ coṟkaḷ mikac citaivupaṭum. ciṟumakār rāmā eṉpavaṉai āmā veṉṟaḻaippar. iv vuṇmai nāṭōṟum paḻakkattā laṟiyaṟpālatēyām. muṉ moḻikku mutalākā  veṉappaṭṭa eṭṭu meyyeḻuttuk kaḷum nā mēlaṇṇattaic ceṉṟu toṭum muyaṟciyāṟ piṟappaṉavām. nāvai mēlē cērtti uccarikkum ammuyaṟci piḷḷaipparuvattē tōṉṟāmaiyiṉ, ap paruvattē piṟakkuñ coṟkaḷellām avveḻuttukkaḷai uṭaiyaṉa vākā; paṭaipput toṭakkattiṟ ṟōṉṟiya makkaḷatu nilaimaiyum piḷḷaipparuvattōṭu iyaintatākaliṉ, aññāṉṟu avar ṟappitta coṟkaḷilē av veḻuttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭāvāyiṉa. itaṉāl, tamiḻccoṟkaḷ makkaḷuṭaiya mutaṟparuvattilē tōṉṟi nilavutaluṟṟaṉa veṉpatu eḷitiṟ peṟappaṭṭatu. vaṭamoḻi mutalāṉa maṟṟaimoḻic coṟkaḷ mutalilellām an nāveḻuttukkaḷ niṟṟal aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, avai makkaḷuṭaiya piṟparuvattilē tōṉṟiyavā meṉpatu tuṇiporuḷ. mēlum, ippōtu nūlvaḻakkāyuḷḷa vaṭamoḻi makkaḷāl eññāṉṟum pēcappaṭṭatillai yeṉṟum, itaṟkut tāymoḻiyāṉa āriyamē aṅṅaṉam pēcappaṭṭatoṉṟā meṉṟum ammoḻi yārāyccivalla paṇṭitarkaḷ palarum uraiyā niṉṟār. ivvāṟṟāl, makkaḷuṭaiya muṟ paruvattilē piṟanta tamiḻmoḻi avaruṭaiya piṟ paruvattilē tōṉṟiya vaṭa moḻikkum muṟpaṭṭa toṉṟātal muṭinta vuṇmaiyām. itaṉvirivum tamiḻ mikap paḻaiyamoḻi eṉṉum ñāṉacākara uraiyuṭ kāṭṭiṉām. atu niṟka.
iṉit, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiya virivāṉum mikappaḻaiya toṉṟeṉpatu kāṭṭappaṭum. ippōtu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷuḷ mikappaḻaiyatu tolkāppiyam oṉṟēyām. kuṟaipāṭiṉṟip pākupāṭu naṉiterittu eḻutappaṭum ilakkaṇa nūl tamakkuṭaya moḻi kaḷellām mikac cīrtiruttamuṟṟu nākarikam nirampiya moḻikaḷākumeṉṟu aṟivuṭaiyōr oruppaṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟār. āṅkilamoḻi iññāṉṟu evvaḷavō cīrtiruttam aṭaintum ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ oṉṟāyiṉum kuṟaipāṭiṉṟi eḻutappaṭavillai. tamiḻōṭu iṉamuṭaiya teluṅku malaiyāḷam kaṉṉaṭam mutalāṉa moḻikaḷ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākac cīrtiruttañ ceyappaṭṭu vantum, avaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum itukāṟum ilakkaṇanūl oṉṟēṉuñ cevvaiyāka eḻutappaṭavillai. ilakkaṇanūl cevvaiyāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa moḻikaḷ ivvulakil ettaṉaiyuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu eṇṇippārkkum vaḻi avai mikac cilavē uḷavātal ellārāṉum eḷitilē yaṟiyappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻ, vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum iru moḻikaḷiṉum eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkaṇanūlkaḷiṉ perumaiyum, nuṇukkamum, niṟaivum ārāyun tōṟum pēriṉpattai viḷaikkiṉṟaṉa. oru moḻiyaic cīrtiruttam paṇṇuvatu oruvariruvarāṉ muṭiyum eḷiyakāriyamaṉṟu. cuḻaṉṟu cellum puyaṟ kāṟṟiṉaiyum, aṇaikaṭantu perukum veḷḷattiṉaiyum iṭaimaṟittu niṟuttiṉum niṟuttalākum; oru moḻiyiṉ virainta celavai aṭakkiyāḷutal yārkkum muṭiyāta toṉṟām. vēta kālattilē eḻutappaṭṭa āriya moḻi kāviya kālattil evvaḷavō māṟutalaṭaintatu. pōvulp kēṭimāṉ kālattil naṭaipeṟṟa āṅkilamoḻi cācar kālattilum, cācar kālattiya moḻi cekappiriyar kālattilum, cekappiriyar kālattiya moḻi ṭeṉicaṉ kālattilum ettaṉai ettaṉaiyō māṟutal aṭaintaṉa. iṅṅaṉaṅ kālaṅkaṭōṟum oru moḻiyiṉ muṉṉuruvan tiripeyti varumāyiṉ am moḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkai yaṟintu ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟutal mika aritām. atu kālaṅkaṭōṟum vēṟupaṭṭu varumāyiṉ ataṉai vaḻaṅkum makkaḷ taṅkaruttaṟivikka eḷitāṉa karuvi kāṇamāṭṭātu mika iṭarppaṭuvar. itupaṟṟiyē āṅkila aracuṭaiyār tammuṭaiya moḻiyiṉai oḻuṅka kaṭavātu nilaipeṟuvittaṟ poruṭṭuk kalviccālaikaḷ palappala viyaṟṟi moḻiyiṉait tiruttamuṟak kaṟpittuk kalvivaḷam perukki varukiṉṟār. nākarika makkaḷiṭattu naṭaipeṟum moḻikaḷ viraivilē vēṟupaṭutalillai; putiya cila coṟkaḷ piṟariṭamiruntu pōntu cērum ammaṭṭē yaṉṟit tam uruvaviyal peritun tiripeytātu paravi vaḻaṅkum. oru moḻi uruvan tiripaṭaiyātu nilainiṟṟaṟku vēṇṭuma muyaṟci eṉṉai eṉṟu āyumvaḻi ataṉkaṇ ovvoru collum paṇṭu toṭṭu vāṅkumāṟum, ac coṟkaḷ tōṉṟuṅkāṟ peṟṟa kāraṇamum, avai vākkiyaṅkaḷil toṭarpuṟṟu niṉṟu poruḷ aṟivikkumāṟum, avaṟṟōṭu kalantu vaḻaṅkum piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ ivai eṉṟalum iṉitu aṟiyappaṭṭu varutalēyām. ivai ivvāṟu aṟiyappaṭutal kaṟṟōriṭat taṉṟi maṟṟai yōriṭattu illaiyālōveṉiṉ; ōr ūril kaṟṟaṟivuṭaiyār oruvar iruppiṉum avarāl avvūrār ellārum tiruttamaṭaivār. tāḻi niṟaiyap peyta tīmpālil kuṟṟiya uṟaittuḷi mikac ciṟitāyiṉum, aḵtu ap pāl muḻuvataiyum tīñcuvait tayirākat tiripu ceytalpōlak kaṟṟār oruvarēyāyiṉum avar tammaic cūḻntār aṉaivarum nalam peṟac ceyvar. eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, avar tampāl vantaṇukuvōrkkut tiruntiya coṟporuḷai aṟivippa, aṟivikkappaṭṭa avar māṇavar tām avaṟṟaip piṟarkku aṟivippa, ap piṟar avaṟṟai vēṟu piṟarkku aṟivippa, iṅṅaṉam ōriṭattut tōṉṟiya alai taṭākattiṉ nāṟpuṟaṅkaḷiṉum pala alaikaḷait tōṟṟuvittu naṭaipeṟac ceytalpōla ak kaṟṟaṟivuṭaiyār tām peṟṟa attiruttaṅkaḷellām avaraṟintum aṟiyāmalum yāṇṭum paravikkoṇṭēyirukkum. ivvāṟu ulakattil naṭaipeṟum oḻukkaṅkaḷellām kaṟṟārāṟ cīrtiruntap peṟṟu varutal paṟṟiyē ulakameṉpa tuyarntōr māṭṭē eṉṉum tiruvākku eḻuntatu.
iṉi, enta nāṭṭil toṉṟutoṭṭuk kaṟṟōr mikutiyāyp palki varuvarō anta nāṭu vaḷam perukum; ataṉkaṇ uḷḷār ellārum kalvi maṇam peṟṟu aṟivu viḷakkamuṭaiyarāvar; avar vaḻaṅkum moḻi mikunta ciṟappuṭaiyatāy nilaipeṟum. tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ paṇṭaik kālattuk kaṟṟaṟivuṭaiya cāṉṟōr kaṇakkiṉṟi yiruntā reṉpataṟku avarkaḷāl tiruvāymalarntaruḷappaṭṭa aḷaviṟanta nūlkaḷum, aḷaviṟanta pāṭṭukkaḷumē cāṉṟām. puṟanāṉūṟu mutalāṉa paḻaiya caṅkat tokainūlkaḷaik kāṇpēmāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟ peṟutaṟkariya muḻumaṇi pōṉṟa ariya periya ceyyuṭkaḷai iyaṟṟiyaruḷiya apperuntakai yāḷar pēraṟivum peruntokaiyum peritum viyakkaṟ pālaṉavā yirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē namatu centamiḻmoḻi evvaḷavu tiruttamāka vaḻaṅkap paṭṭulāviyatu! annāṭkaḷilirunta kaṟṟōr nuṇṇaṟivu evvaḷavu teḷivāka avariyaṟṟiya pākkaḷilē tuḷumpi oḷi vīcukiṉṟatu! appōtirunta naṉmakkaḷellārum evvaḷavu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntaṉarkaḷ! eṉṟu niṉaikka niṉaikka em uḷḷam varaikaṭanta iṉpattāṟ poṅkukiṉṟatu. akkālattil eḻutappaṭṭa teyvat tirukkuṟaḷukku iṇaiyāṉa nūl ivvulakattil uṇṭō? aḵtu uyarvu oppillā uvarā amiḻtamaṉṟō! uḷḷaṉa uḷḷapaṭi kūṟiya muḻukkāppiyaṅkaḷāṉa cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai eṉpavaṟṟiṉmuṉ talaiyeṭuttu niṟkum vēṟu kāppiyaṅkaḷ ivvulakil enta moḻiyilēṉum uḷavā? illaiyē! makkaḷ meyyuṇarviṉ iyaṟkaiyiṉaip pakuttu viḷakkiya iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai pōṉṟa nūl iv virikaṭalulakiṉum illaiyē. kalittokaiyiluḷḷa iyaṟkaip poruḷ nuṭpamum, palatiṟappaṭṭa makkaḷ vaḻak oḻukka ārāycciyum vēṟu emmoḻi nūlilāyiṉum uḷḷaṉavā? maṉu mutaliya aṟanūlkaḷellām nālaṭiyārai okkumō? ā! akkālattut tōṉṟiya tirucciṟappampalak kōvaiyār eṉṉum avvoru nūlai nōkkiṉum tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ amaitiyum ciṟappum iṉitu pulappaṭumē. naṇparkāḷ! iṅṅaṉañ cīrtiruttamuṟṟu nilāviya iccentamiḻai vaḻaṅkiya makkaḷ ettuṇai nākarikamuṭaiyarā yiruntaṉarāvar!
iṉi innūlkaḷukkellām mika muṟpaṭṭa tolkāppiya nūṟperumaiyaic ciṟitu ārāyntiṭuvē māyiṉ tamiḻar varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉṉum mikka nākarikam peṟṟu vāḻntāreṉal teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. tolkāppiyam muṟkūṟiya tamiḻnūlkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiṟkum muṉṟōṉṟiya tātalai oru ciṟitu ārāyvām. itu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pāṇiṉi muṉivar iyaṟṟiya aṭṭāttiyāyi eṉṉum ariya periya ilakkaṇa nūlukkum muntiyateṉṟu nāṭṭavē, itaṉ kālam at tamiḻ nūlkaḷukkellām muṟpaṭṭatātalum naṉku peṟappaṭum. ākaliṉ mutaliṟ pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālam īṇṭuc curuṅka vakuppām. pāṇiṉi muṉivar tāmiyaṟṟiya avvilakkaṇa nūlil ivvintiya nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi avaṟṟiṉ peyark kellām ilakkaṇam vakutturaikkiṉṟār. perumpālum avarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷellām pañcāp nāṭṭilum apkāṉittāṉattilum irukkiṉṟaṉa. intiyāvil teṟkup pakkaṅkaḷil avarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ kacca,1 avanti2 kōcala3, karūca,4 kaliṅka5 mutaliyaṉa vām. ivai yellām vintiyamalaikku vaṭakkēyuḷḷaṉa. vintiya malaikkut teṟkē uḷḷa nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷil oṉṟāyiṉum avarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭavillai. ākavē, pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālattu āriyamakkaḷ vintiyamalaikku vaṭakkē uḷḷa nāṭukaḷil iruntāreṉpatum, am malaiyaik kaṭantu ippakuti vantilareṉpatum iṉitu peṟappaṭuvaṉām.
iṉip, pāṇiṉī yattiṟku vārttikai urai eḻutiya kāttiyāyaṉār vintiyamalaikkut teṟkē uḷḷa nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. ōr iṭattiṉ peyarpaṟṟi avviṭattiṟ piṟanta makkaṭkup peyar varumāṟṟaic cuṭṭip pāṇiṉimuṉivar viti kūṟip pañcālattil tōṉṟiṉavaṉ pāñcālaṉ, cālvattiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉavaṉ cālvēyaṉ eṉpaṉavāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār. īṇṭu iccūttirattil vēṟu cila viṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟuraittuk kāttiyāyaṉar pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉuñcoṟ pāṇiṉiyāṟ collappaṭa villai; pāṇṭu kuṭiyil allatu pāṇṭu nāṭṭiṟ piṟantōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ eṉṟu putiya ōr uraivākkiya muñ cērtteḻutiṉār. iṉṉum ivvāṟē cōḻar makiṣmat eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum ak kāttiyāyaṉarāl ārāynturaikkap paṭṭaṉa.
iṉip, pāṇiṉīyattiṟku māpāṭiya muraitta patañcaliyār mākiṣmatī vaitarppa kāñcīpura kēraḷa mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum ārāyntu uraiyuraittār. atuvēyu maṉṟit tammuraiyuṭ kāttiyāyaṉar vārttikavurai pāṭa pētaṅkaḷ pala uḷavātalum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. kāttiyāyaṉarurai pala pāṭapētaṅkaḷ uṭaittāyt tiripeytutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cellumākaliṉ, ap pāṭapētaṅkaḷait tammuraiyuṭ kuṟitta āciriyar patañcali yārkku munnūṟu nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ ak kāttiyāyaṉar iruntārākaṟpālār. maṟṟup patañcaliyār iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyirattu aimpattāṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ iruntāreṉa vaṭanūl varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷ niṟuviṉā rākaliṉ, kāttiyāyaṉar kālam 2350 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṟpaṭṭa teṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉip pāṇiṉi muṉivarāṟ collappaṭāta putiya ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ cila kāttiyāyaṉarāṟ collap paṭṭiruppatu koṇṭu, avvitikaḷ peṟutaṟkuriya col vaḻakkukaḷ cila pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālattilillai eṉpatūum, avai kāttiyāyaṉar kālattē tāṉ tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatūum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. atuvēyu maṉṟip pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālattu naṭaipeṟṟa coṟkaḷ pala kāttiyāyaṉar kālattu vaḻakku vīḻntoḻintaṉa. iṅṅaṉam moḻi tiripeytutaṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cellumākaliṉ pāṇiṉi muṉivarkkum kāttiyāyaṉarkkum iṭaiyil virinta kālam nāṉūṟu āṇṭiṟkuk kuṟaiyātākum. eṉavē, pāṇiṉi muṉivar iṟṟaikku 2800 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉiruntā reṉpatu iṉitu peṟappaṭum.
iṉi, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār collatikārattil tamiḻc collilakkaṇaṅ kaḷellām muṟṟavuṅ kūṟiṉā rāyiṉum, accoṟkaḷ evvāṟu piṟantaṉa eṉṟu āyntu avaitammai yellām pakuttup pakutikaḷāka aṭakkik kāṭṭiṉārillai. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, ovvoru colluṅ kāraṇam paṟṟiyē tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟum, ak kāraṇam aṟivuṭaiyārkkallatu ēṉaiyōrkku viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā veṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭu moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā eṉṟu cūttirañ ceytaruḷiṉār. ivvāṟṟāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷaip pakuti, vikuti mutaliyaṉavākap pakuttu ārāynturaikkum muṟai tolkāppiyaṉār nūleḻutiya ñāṉṟu tamiḻāciriyarkkut teriyātirunta teṉṟē tuṇiyappaṭum. maṟṟup pāṇiṉi muṉivar tāmiyaṟṟiya aṭṭāttiyāyiyil taṅkālattu vaḻaṅkiya pallāyiram āriya moḻikaḷai yellām pakuti vikutikaḷākap pakuttārāyntu avaṟṟai yellām tātupātattiṟ cillāyiram pakutikaḷilē aṭakkiṉār. tolkāppiyaṉār pāṇiṉi muṉivar kālatti lēṉum, allatu ataṟkup piṉṉēṉum iruntaṉarāyiṉ ilakkaṇa ārāyccikku iṉṟiyamaiyāp peruñ ciṟapṉitāṉa colvaralāṟṟu muṟaiyait tavaṟātu kūṟiyiruppar. pāṇiṉi muṉivarkkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattiliruntavarāṉa pavaṇantiyār iccol varalāṟṟu muṟaiyait tamatu naṉṉūlil taḻuvik kūṟiṉamai aṟiyavallārkku yām uraittatu mikavum porutta muṭaittātal iṉitu viḷaṅkum. ākavē, colvaralāṟṟu muṟai terinturaitta pāṇiṉi muṉivarkkum, ammuṟai taṅkālat tillāmaiyāl ataṉai uraiyātu viṭutta tolkāppiyaṉārkkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālam mikap peritātal vēṇṭum. pāṇiṉi muṉivarkku nāṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉṉē āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iruntaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷumvaḻi, iṟṟaikku mūvāyirattirunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ avvāciriyar kālam kaṇikkappaṭumeṉṟu koḷka.
iṉi, maṟṟumoru vāṟṟāl tolkāppiyaṉār kālam āyumvaḻi atu nālāyira āṇṭukaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭateṉṟu nāṭṭappaṭum. tolkāppiyam paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭumuṉ eḻutappaṭṭatu; akkālattiṟ kumariyāṟu teṉṟicaiyil ōṭik koṇṭiruntatu. itu vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari eṉṉum paṉampāraṉār pāyiravuraiyāṉum, ataṟkurai kaṇṭa iḷampūraṇar mutalāṉa uraiyāciriyar uraiyāṉum naṉkuṇarappaṭum. iv yāṟukaḷum ivaṟṟaic cūḻnta nilaṅkaḷum kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭatu iṟṟaikku nālāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉeṉṟu ilaṅkaip pautta vamicāvaḷiyilē kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭṭa kālamum, viviliya nūlil kuṟikkappaṭṭa nōvā eṉpavar kālamum mikavum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, tolkāppiyaṉār nālāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉiruntā reṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟuvām.
iṉi, ittuṇaip paḻai kālattē eḻutappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṉum tamiḻk kaḷañciyattil tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷa poruḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷ īṇṭu muṟṟavum eṭuttuka kāṭṭal ēlāmaiyiṉ, ovvōratikārattilum oṉṟiraṇṭu eṭutturaippām. eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutalilēyē avvāciriyar tamiḻ  oli eḻuttukkaḷai muṟaiyē niṟuttik kūṟiya tiṟam peritum viyakkaṟ pālatām. mūṭi irunta vāyait tiṟanta māttiraiyāṉē akara oli iyaṟkaiyāyt tōṉṟu taliṉ ataṉai muṉṉum, ataṉpiṉ ikaram nāvāṟ piṟattaliṉ ataṉaip piṉṉum, ataṉpiṉ avvōcai itaḻ vaḻiyē ukaramāyc ceṉṟu aḻitaliṉ ataṉai ataṉ piṉṉumāka niṟuttiyaruḷiṉār. muṟpiṟakkum ōcai yellām kuṟuki yirukkumātalāṉum, avvōcai piṟkaṇattiṟ ṟoṭarppaṭṭu nīḷuvatē neṭilāmātalāṉum muṉṉē a, i, u eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṉ nīṭṭamāṉa ā, ī, ū eṉpavaṟṟaip piṉṉumāka vaittār. ēṉaiya ōcaikaḷiṉ muṟaiyum īṇṭuraippiṟ perukum. avaiyellām āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiya ruraiyiṟ kāṇka. aṟṟēl aṅṅaṉamāka, ivveḻuttumuṟai vaṭamoḻi mutalāṉa piṟamoḻikaḷiṉum kāṇappaṭuvatāka itaṉait tamiḻukkē varaintu colliya teṉṉaiyeyaṉiṉ; - vaṭamoḻiyai oḻittu oḻinta intiya moḻikaḷilellām kāṇappaṭum immuṟai, tamiḻ āriyattiṉ muṟaikaṇṭu ceytu koḷḷap paṭṭatākaliṉ aḵtīṇṭu ārāyaṟpāṟṟaṉṟu. maṟṟu vaṭamoḻiyilimmuṟai paṇṭutoṭṭuk kāṇappaṭutaliṉ aḵtoṉṟē īṇṭārāyaṟ pālatām. tamiḻar ceyta muṟaiyaik kaṇṭu āriyar tām ammuṟai kāṭṭiṉarō, allatu āriyar ceyta muṟaiyaik kaṇṭu tamiḻartām atu kāṭṭiṉarō eṉpatutāṉ īṇṭu uṟuticeyyaṟpālatām. āriyar ivvintiyā tēcattiṟ pukumuṉṉē ammuṟai kaṇṭaṟintaṉarāyiṉ aḵtu avarkkē uriyatākum; avar intiyāviṟ pukunta piṉ aṅkirunta tamiḻar ataṉai eṭuttāṇṭu koṇṭārāvar. maṟṟu āriyar intiyāviṟ pukumuṉ ammuṟai kāṇātiruntu pukuntapiṉ atu kaṇṭaṉareṉṟu urai niṟuvappaṭumāyiṉ, tamiḻarkkē ammuṟai uriyatāku meṉpatum ataṉaiyē āriyar eṭuttāṇṭu koṇṭaṉa reṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
iṉi āriyar intiyāviṟ pukumuṉṉē ammuṟai aṟintārillai; aḵtaṟintatu uṇmaiyāyiṉ avvāriyap piriviṉarāy airōppāk kaṇṭattiṉ teṉṟicai, mēṟṟicai, vaṭamēṟ ṟicaikaḷiṟ pukunta ēṉai āriya cātiyār vaḻaṅkiya ilattiṉ, kirīkku, ṭiyuṭāṉik, cilāvik mutaliya āriya moḻikaḷiṉum ammuṟai kāṇappaṭutalvēṇṭum. avaṟṟi lellām uyir eḻuttu meyyeḻuttukkaḷ oru muṟaiyumiṉṟip palavāṟu mayaṅkik kiṭattaliṉ, ammuṟai āriya moḻik kuriyataṉ ṟeṉpatu aṅkaiyuḷ nellipōṟ ṟeḷiyappaṭum. maṟṟu avvāriyar intiyāviṟ pukuntapiṉ tamakku muṉṉē aṅku vāḻntu varun tamiḻ āciriyar kaṇṭa avveḻuttu muṟaiyai eṭuttāṇṭu koṇṭār eṉka. ivvuṇmai pautta intiyā eṉṉum varalāṟṟu nūlil irai ṭēviṭ eṉṟa aṟiñarālum naṉku kāṭṭap paṭṭatu.1
atukiṭakka, āriyamoḻi ilattīṉ, kirīkku mutaliyavaṟ ṟōṭu iṉamuṭaittātal yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum. oru moḻi piṟitoṉṟaṉōṭu iṉamuṭait teṉpatu avaṟṟiṉ mutal tōṟṟac coṟkaḷiṉ uruvop pumaiyāl naṉku tuṇiyap paṭumaṉṟē! eṉṉuñ col makaṉaik kuṟikkum. avvāriyat tōṭu iṉamāṉa seṇṭ eṉṉum pāracīka moḻiyilum apporuḷaik kuṟikkum accol hunu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu; ṭiyūṭāṉik moḻiyil cunu eṉavum avvāṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āriyattil makaḷaik kuṟikkum tukitār eṉṉuñ col, seṇṭil tuktar eṉavum, kirīkkil tukātīr eṉavum, ṭiyūṭāṉikil tohtar eṉavum, cilāvikil tuter eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āriyattil uṭaṉ piṟantāṉaik kuṟikkum prātar eṉpatu, ilāvikil praturu eṉavum  seṇṭil prātar eṉavum, kirīkkilprātī eṉavum, ilattīṉil vrāter eṉavum, ṭiyūṭāṉikil prōtar eṉavum, selṭikil prātir eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āriyattilmāṭṭaik kuṟikkum pacū eṉṉuñ col, seṇṭil pacu eṉavum, ilattīṉil pika eṉavum, ṭiyūṭāṉikil vaihu eṉavum, cilāvikil piku eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āriyattil āṭṭaikkuṟikkum āvi eṉuñcol, kirīkkil ōyī eṉavum, ilattīṉil ōvi eṉavum, ṭiyūṭāṉikil avi eṉavum, cilāvikil ōvikā eṉavum, selṭikil ōy eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āriyattil pāmpaik kuṟikkum aki eṉpatu, seṇṭil aṣi eṉavum, kirīkkil eki eṉavum, ilattīṉil aṅkuyi eṉavum, ṭiyūṭāṉikil aṅk eṉavum, cilāvikil aṅkī eṉavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉṉum ivvāṟē varuvaṉavellām kāṭṭappukiṉ ic coṟpoḻivu varampiṉṟi mika viriyumeṉa añci ittuṇaiyē īṇṭaikkup pōtumeṉa niṟuttukiṉṟōm. ik kāṭṭiya vāṟṟāl āriyamoḻi seṇṭ, kirīkku, ilattīṉ mutalāṉa moḻikaḷōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaittātal naṉku teḷiyappaṭum. iṉi āriyattil vaḻaṅkum cūnu, tukitār, prātar, pacū, āvi, aki eṉṉum accoṟkaḷukkut, tamiḻil muṟaiyē makaṉ, makaḷ, uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ, māṭu, āṭu, pāmpu eṉa vaḻaṅkuñ coṟkaḷ oruvāṟṟālum uru voppumai peṟātu niṟṟalum īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatām.
iṉik, kirēkkar, urōmar, āṅkilar, āriyar mutalāṉa āriya cātiyār āriyar intiya nāṭṭiṟ pukumuṉṉē ōcaikaḷ piṟakkummuṟai aṟintu eḻuttukkaḷai muṉpiṉṉāka niṟuttiṉa rāyiṉ ammuṟai avvāriya moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiluṅ kāṇak kiṭakkum. maṟṟu ammuṟai avvāriya moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭāmal akarattiṉmuṉ pakaramum ataṉpiṉ cakaramumāka iṅṅaṉam orumuṟaiyumiṉṟi niṟuttappaṭṭuk kiṭattalāl, āriyar ammuṟai muṉ aṟiyārāy intiyāviṟ pukuntapiṉ aṟintāreṉpatu iṉitu nāṭṭappaṭum.
iṉi, āriyar varutaṟku muṉṉarē tamiḻar mikka nākarika muṭaiyarā yiruntaṉareṉpatu irukkuvē tattiṉālēyē iṉitu aṟiyappaṭṭatoṉṟām. āriyar cintu natikkaraiyil vantu kuṭiyēṟiya kālattil, uḷnāṭṭil irunta tamiḻar eḻuvakai araṇmaṉaikaḷum, toṇṇūṟu kōṭṭaikaḷum uṭaiyarāy vāḻntaṉareṉa irukkuvētam1 urai tarukiṉṟatu. iṟṟaikku nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻar aḻakiṟ ciṟanta eḻunilai māṭaṅkaḷum, uyarnta kaṟkōṭṭaikaḷum kaṭṭuvittu vāḻntaṉarāyiṉ, avvariya periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ amaippataṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāp peruñ celvavaḷamum, avai tammait tiruttamuṟak kaṭṭuvittu muṭippataṟku uriya nūl uṇarvum, avaṟṟuḷ naṭattappaṭum palatiṟappaṭṭa ilaukika karumaṅkaḷum uṭaiyarāy iruntārātal teṟṟeṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. ittuṇaip periya nākarika vāḻkkai uṭaiyarāyirunta tamiḻar tamatu nākarika vāḻkkai iṉitu naṭaipeṟutaṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum tamiḻ moḻiyiṉai ilakkaṇa ilakkiya amaitiyōṭu muṟṟak kaṟṟuvantā reṉpatūum, itaṉāl nilainiṟuttappaṭum muṭiporuḷām. ākavē, ōriṭattum nilaipeṟamāṭṭātu tiritaru vāḻkkai yuṭaiyarāy intiyāviṉuṭ pukunta āriyar kaṅkaiyāṟṟuk karaip pakkaṅkaḷil nilaipeṟṟa vāḻkkai naṭāttic ceṅkōl ōcciya tamiḻmakkaḷāl neṟippaṭuttap paṭṭa ilakkaṇamuṟai kaṇṭu tāmum uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷai muṟaippaṭuttiṉāreṉṟu tuṇika.
āriyar varuñāṉṟu, tamiḻar araciyal muṟai piḻaiyātu vāḻntaṉarākaliṉ avarai āriyar acurar eṉṟu peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉār. irukku vētattiṉ mutal oṉpatu maṇṭilaṅkaḷ muḻuvatūum acura eṉṉuñ col valiya allatu atikāramuṭaiya eṉṉum poruḷil uriccollāy vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varakiṉṟateṉṟum, acurar eṉpataṟku ataṉāl talaivar eṉṉum poruḷ peṟappaṭukiṉṟa teṉṟum urōmēṣ cantiratattarum iṉitu viḷakkiṉār. ñimiṟu eṉpatu miñiṟu eṉavum, tacai eṉpatu catai eṉavum, viciṟi eṉpatu civiṟi eṉavum eḻuttu nilaimāṟi varutalpōla aracu eṉṉun tamiḻccol acara eṉa nilaimāṟi irukkuvētattuḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.1 tamakku etirē nākarikam nirampi viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tamiḻa aracarait tamakkun talaivarāka oruppaṭṭup paṇṭai āriyamakkaḷ acurar eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉār. piṟkālattu vaṭamoḻiyil maṭṭum acurar eṉṉum accolliṉ uyarccipporuḷai māṟṟi ataṟku arakkareṉap poruḷ kaṭṭiviṭṭārkaḷ. maṟṟu irukkuvētamō araciyalneṟi tiṟampāta tamiḻaracaraic cuṭṭi acurar eṉa mikavum pārāṭṭi vaḻaṅkiyatu. ittamiḻaracar taṅkīḻ vāḻvārkkellām mika iṉiyarāy oḻukiyatupaṟṟi, ivarkkum ivar kuṭumpattārkkum araciṉiyar eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. araciṉiyar eṉṉum iccoṟ ṟoṭaraiyē irukkuvēta kālattu āriyamakkaḷ rājanyar eṉat tamakkēṟṟavāṟu tirittu vaḻaṅkiṉār. araciṉiyār eṉappaṭum paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻaracar nuṇṇaṟivu nūluṇarcciyāṟ ṟattuvaṅkaḷ palavum āyntu muṭi poruṭ ṭērcciyuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉāreṉpatūum appaḻaiya āriya nūlāṟ peṟappaṭum uṇmaiyām. itaṉaip piṉṉē virittu viḷakkuvām.
iṉi ituvēyumaṉṟit tamiḻ makkaḷ tām uraiyāṭuṅ kāṟ ṟōṉṟum ōcaikaḷaip palvakai eḻuttuk kaḷāl iṭṭeḻutip paṇṭē nākarika mutircci uṭaiyarāyu miruntaṉar. āriyarō tampāṟ piṟanta ōcaikaḷai eḻuttiliṭṭuk kāṭṭum vakai aṟiyāmaiyiṉāl, tam muṉṉōrāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa pāṭṭukkaḷai neṭṭurup paṇṇutaṟkuk kiṭaikūṭṭip payiṉṟu vantār. avvaḻakkam iṉṟum palar oruṅku kūṭiyiruntu vētam ōtumāṟṟāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam eḻuttukkaḷiṉṟi veṟum ōcai māttiraiyāy vētaṅkaḷ avarāṟ payilappaṭṭu vantamaiyālaṉṟē avai eḻutākkiḷavi eṉaṟu peyar peṟuvaṉavāyiṉa. allatūum, vaṭamoḻikkeṉṟē ōreḻuttu illāmai yiṉālētāṉ, vaṭanāṭukaḷiluḷḷār makārāṭṭirat tiṟkuriya tēva nākara eḻuttukkaḷāṉum, teṉṉāṭu kaḷiluḷḷār tamiḻukkuriya kirantākkaraṅkaḷālum, āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷār teluṅkiṟ kuriya teluṅkeḻuttuk kaḷāṉum vaṭanūlkaḷai eḻututalum patippittaluñ ceytu pōtarukiṉṟār. paṇṭai nāḷ āriyar eḻuttaṟiyāmaiyiṉ tammuṭaiya paḻaiya nūlkaḷaip pātukāttu vaḻaṅkaṟ poruṭṭut tiruttamāka avaṟṟai ōtutaṟ kuriya muṟaikaḷai ellām pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷil mikaviritteḻutiṉār. eḻuttaṟintaṉarāyiṉ avvā ṟellām avaṟṟai ōtutaṟkuriya muṟaikaḷai mikavarunti viritteḻutal vēṇṭappaṭāteṉka. niṉaikkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷai yuṭaiya miruti eṉṉuñ collum kēṭkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷai yuṭaiya curuti eṉṉuñ collum avvaṭanūlkaḷukkup peyarāy amaintataṉai uṟṟunōkku miṭattu avai niṉaikkappaṭṭuṅ kēṭkap paṭṭum vantaṉavē yallatu eḻuttiliṭṭu eḻutappaṭavillai yeṉpatu pōtarum.
iṉi āriyar intiyāviṟ pukuntapiṉ tamiḻaruṭaiya nākarika mutircciyiṉaiyum, avar tāṅ karutiya poruḷai eḻuttiliṭṭup poṟittaluṅkaṇṭu tāmum tammuṭaiya pāṭṭukkaḷaip paṇ aṭaivupaṭa vakutta ñāṉṟu eḻutumuṟai kaṇṭaṟintār. itu kōlṭ ṭakkar eṉṉum paṇṭitar viḷakkumāṟṟāṉum naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. iraiṭēviṭ paṇṭitarum intiyāviluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ āriyarkkuriya valla veṉṟum, avai tirāviṭa viyāpārikaḷāṟ koṇṭuvantu tarappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum kūṟumāṟṟāṉum1 avai tamiḻarkkē uriyaveṉpatu iṉitu peṟappaṭum.
iṉi, varalāṟṟu nūl aṟiñaril orucārār piṉī ciyareṉṉum nākarika cātiyārōṭu vāṇikam naṭāttiya tamiḻar avariṭamiruntu eḻutumuṟai kaṟṟup piṉ ataṉai intiyāveṅkum parappiṉār eṉak kūṟukiṉṟār. ivaruraikkum ivvuraiyōṭu yām oru vāṟṟāl iṇaṅku talum piṟitoru vāṟṟāl iṇaṅkāmaiyum uṭaiyēm. yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum:
iṟṟaikku mūvāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ mēṟṟicai yiluḷḷa piṉīciyar eṉṉum vakuppār teṉṉintiyāviṉ mēṟkē malaiyāḷak karaiyiṉum, kiḻakkē uvari, koṟkai eṉṉuṅ kaṭaṟṟuṟaip paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉum vantiṟaṅkit tamiḻarōṭu vāṇikam naṭāttiṉār. avar tam nāṭṭukkut tirumpippōm pōtu yāṉaik kompukaḷ, tōkai mayilkaḷ, kuraṅkukaḷ, cantaṉak kaṭṭaikaḷ, vilai uyarnta muttukaḷ, arici, karuvāppaṭṭai mutaliya carakkukaḷai ēṟṟik koṇṭupōyiṉār. avarkaḷ cālamaṉ eṉṉum mēl nāṭṭaracaṉ kālattiṟ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāy vantaṉareṉṟu īpurumoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭa viviliya nūlāl naṉkaṟi kiṉṟōm. annūlil tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭaṟṟuṟaip paṭṭiṉamāṉa uvari eṉpatu ōyir2 eṉavum, avar tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu ēṟṟik koṇṭupōṉa poruḷkaḷāṉa, mayil, cantaṉam, arici eṉpavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ tōkai, aṉukam, arici eṉavum avviviliya nūlil eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.3 cālamaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṉ iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ceṅkōlōcciṉā ṉeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūl aṟiñark kellām oppamuṭintamaiyiṉ, avaṉ kālattil tamiḻnāṭu pōntu tamiḻarōṭu vāṇikam naṭāttiya piṉīciyar tām payaṉ paṭuttivanta eḻutu muṟaiyait tamiḻarkkuṅ kaṟpittār. āyiṉ, tamiḻar avar varutaṟkumuṉ eḻutumuṟai aṟiyārō veṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu. piṉīciyar varutaṟku muṉṉun tamiḻar ammuṟai naṉkaṟintār eṉpatu tēṟṟamām. yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār eḻuttatikāram eṉa oṉṟu vakuttukkoṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇattaic cevvaṉē kūṟukiṉṟamai yāṉum, eḻuttukkaḷ illāviṭiṉ aṅṅaṉa mellām ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟutal ākāmaiyāṉum uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē eṉṉuñ cūttirattil makara eḻuttu muṉ nāḷil eḻutappaṭṭa varivaṭivum, meyyiṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal eṉṉuñ cūttirattil meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟṟalum ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyumaṟṟē eṉpataṉāl ekara okara eḻuttukkaḷum avvāṟē paṇṭai nāḷil puḷḷi peṟṟu niṉṟamaiyum puḷḷiyillā ellā meyyum, uruvuru vāki akaramoṭu uyirttalum, ēṉai uyirōṭu uruvutirintu uyirttalum, āyīriyala uyirttalāṟē eṉpataṉkaṇ meyyeḻuttukkaḷ uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi varivaṭivil uruvu tiritalum iṉiteṭuttu ōtiṉārākalāṉum, uraiyāciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar uruvutirin tuyirttalāvatu mēluṅ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟuṅ kōṭupeṟṟum puḷḷi peṟṟum puḷḷiyuṅkōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟum uyirttalām. ki.kī mutaliyaṉa mēl vilaṅkupeṟṟaṉa. ku kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke kē mutaliyaṉa kōṭupeṟṟaṉa. kā ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷipeṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyai ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyai vaḷaitteḻutiṉār. ko kō ṅo ṅō mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟaṉa eṉṟu urai virittuk kūṟutalāṉum tamiḻar tamakkeṉa vēṟeḻut tuṭaiyarā yiruntaṉa reṉpatu tuṇiporuḷām.
aṟṟēl, piṉīciyarāl tarappaṭṭa eḻuttuk kaḷukkum tamiḻarkkē uriya eḻuttukkaḷukkum vēṟupāṭu eṉṉai eṉiṉ; - piṉīciyarkkuriya eḻuttukkaḷellām valatukaip puṟattiliruntu iṭatukaippakkamāy eḻutappaṭuvaṉavām;1 tamiḻarkkuriyaṉavō iṭatukaip puṟattiruntu valatu pakkamāy eḻutappaṭuvaṉavām. ivvēṟu pāṭṭuṭaṉ, avar koṇṭu vantaṉa uruvattāṉum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟāvaṉavām. ekupati nāṭṭil eḻuppiya tūpikaḷiṉ mēṟ poṟikkap paṭṭirukkum cittira eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟun tirittuc ceytu koṇṭaṉatām piṉīciyark kuriya eḻuttukkaḷ; maṟṟut tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷō veṉiṉ, akkaṭiyar eṉṉuñ cātiyār pallāyira māṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē vaḻaṅkiya muḷai eḻuttuk2 kaḷiṉiṉṟun tōṉṟi naṭaipeṟuvaṉavām. akkaṭiyar eṉṉuñ cātiyārum paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻaruma oruvarē yeṉpatu tiruvāḷar paṇṭitar cavarirāyaravar kaḷāṟ cittāntatīpikaiyil iṉitu viḷakkappaṭṭatu; ataṉ virivu piṉṉē kāṭṭutum.
iṉip, pautta camayam viḷakkiya acōka maṉṉaṉ kālattiṟ cetukkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ iruvakaip paṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟu valatupuṟattiruntu iṭatu pakkamākavum, oṉṟu iṭatupuṟattiruntu valatupakkamāyum eḻutappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa3. muṉṉaiyatu kapurttakiric cilācātaṉaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu; itu vaṭa acōkalipi eṉṟu collappaṭum. piṉṉaiyatu acōka vēntaṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ perumpālaṉa vaṟṟiluṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu; itu teṉ acōkalipi eṉṟu collappaṭum. vaṭa acōkalipi intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pākaṅkaḷil maṭṭum aruki vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. teṉ acōkalipi intiyāviṟ perumpālum eṅkum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ākaiyāl, teṉ acōkalipi intiyāvilēyē piṟanta eḻuttukkaḷāku meṉpatu naṉku peṟappaṭum. eṉṉai? puṟanāṭṭiliruntu vanta eḻuttukkaḷ intiyāviluḷḷa intiyamakkaḷ ellārkkum viḷaṅkāmaiyiṉ avai ap puṟanāṭṭai yaṭutta intiya ellaiyil maṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa; uḷnāṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷ yārkkum pulappaṭumātalāl avai intiyā veṅkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. iṅṅaṉan, teṉ acōkalipi intiyāviṟkē uriya teṉpataṉai ivviyalpu muṟṟavum ārāynta tāma, kaṉṉiṅkām eṉṉum kalveṭṭu ārāycci aṟiñar iruvarum naṉku viḷakkiṉār.
iṉit, teṉ acōkalipi eṉpatu tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ tiripēyaṉṟi vēṟeṉṟu kūṟutal ākāmaiyāl, atu tamiḻarkkē uriya eḻuttāmeṉṟu nāṭṭappaṭum. vaṭa acōkalipiyum tamiḻar vaḻiyāyp peṟappaṭṭa piṉīciyar eḻuttēyallatu piṟitillai. ivvavāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ iruvakaip pākupāṭum poruntak kāṭṭappaṭṭamaiyāl intiyāvil vaḻaṅkappaṭum eḻuttup piṉīciyariṭamiruntu pōntateṉṟu uraippār uraiyum, aṟṟaṉṟu aḵtu intiyāvilēyē piṟantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉ ṟuraippār uraiyum tammuḷ muraṇāmai kāṇka. piṉīciyariṭamiruntu pōntatellām intiya vaṭamēṟkellaiyil aruki vaḻaṅkum vaṭa acōkalipiyē yākumeṉpatūum, intiyā vilēyē tōṉṟip paṇṭutoṭṭu naṭaipeṟuvatu tamiḻavaṭṭeḻuttiṉ tiripāṉa teṉṉacōkalipiyē yākumeṉpatūum pākupaṭuttu viḷaṅka uṇartal varalāṟṟu nūl vallārkku iṉṟiyamaiyāta kaṭamaiyām. ivvuṇmai tēṟātu avaiyiraṇṭiṉaiyum oṉṟeṉak kūṟi muṭipuraittal oru ciṟitum poruntāteṉka.
iṉi, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār collatikāra mutalilēyē ulakiyaṟ poruḷkaḷai uyartiṇai yeṉṟum aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟum pakuttōtiṉār. cuvai, oḷi, ūṟu, ōcai, nāṟṟam eṉṉum aimpoṟiyuṇarvōṭu ivaṟṟaip pakuttuṇarkiṉṟa maṉavaṟivum uṭaiya uyirkaḷellām makkaḷeṉappaṭuvar eṉṟum, immakkaḷē uyartiṇai yāvareṉṟum, aimpoṟi uṇarvu maṭṭum uṭaiya vilaṅku, puḷ mutaliyaṉavum uyirillāta ēṉaic caṭapporuḷumākiya ellām aḵṟiṇaiyāmeṉṟum iṉitu viḷakkic cūttirañ ceytaruḷiṉār. āṇ peṇ eṉṉum pāṟpakuppum uyartiṇaiyil maṭṭum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutaliṉ ap pākupāṭum uyartiṇaikkē kūṟi, ēṉai aḵṟiṇaiyil uyiruḷḷa eṟumpu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ ap pakuppu avvāṟu viḷaṅkāmaiyiṉ ataṟkatu kūṟārāy viṭuttaṉar. em ariya naṇparkāḷ! ippākupāṭṭiṉ arumaiyum, nuṭpamum nāmuṇarā tirukkiṉṟōm. piṟa moḻikaḷil eṅkāyiṉum ittakaiya nuṇpakuppu uḷatā eṉṟu ārāyvōmāyiṉ appōtu itaṉ perumai naṉku teḷiyakkiṭakkum. āṅkila mutalāṉa mēlnāṭṭu moḻiyi leḻutappaṭṭa ilakṇa nūlkaḷai iṭaiviṭātu eḻutteḻuttāy ārāyntu pārppiṉum avaṟṟiṉkaṇ ivvariya periya ilakkaṇap pākupāṭu oru ciṟituṅ kāṇappaṭātu; aṉṟi, intiya  nāṭṭiṟ cīrttipeṟṟa moḻiyāyp payilappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyilēṉum immuṟaiyuṇṭō veṉiṉ āṇṭum itaṉaik kāṇēm iḵtoṉṟō, av vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟporuṭ pākupāṭukaḷ nuṇṇaṟiviṟkuc ciṟitum iyaiyāvāṟāy amaintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. illam vīṭu eṉpaṉa uyirillāta aḵṟiṇaip poruḷkaḷeṉpatum, avai tammai āṇ, peṇ eṉa vaḻaṅkal nakaiyāṭutaṟ kētuvā meṉpatuñ ciṟumakārum aṟivar. iṅṅaṉamākavum, vaṭamoḻiyil kōyil eṉṉuñ col āṇpālākac collappaṭu kiṉṟatu; cālai eṉpatu peṇpāleṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. mayirkkaṟṟai eṉṉum poruḷuṭaiya kaparī eṉpatu peṇpālām, kēcavēca eṉpatu āṇpālām; viruppam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuma `iccā eṉpatu peṇpālām; manōrata eṉpatu aṇ pālām; mālaippoḻutu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. tinānta eṉṉum orucol āṇpālām; cantyā eṉṉum maṟṟoru col peṇpālām; ulakam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum jakatō eṉpatu peṇpālām; lōka eṉpatu āṇpālām; cuvar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum pitti eṉpatu peṇpālām; kuṭya eṉpatu alippālām; kātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum karṇa eṉpatu āṇpālām; ruti eṉpatu peṇpālām; cravaṇa eṉpatualippālām. naṇparkāḷ! īteṉṉa putumai pāruṅkaḷ! caṭapporuḷāṉa ivaiyellām āṇ peṇ eṉak kūṟiṉāl yārukkuttāṉ nakai viḷaiyātu. ivvāṟu coṟkaḷai oru varaṉmuṟaiyumiṉṟit tōṉṟiyavāṟu aṇ peṇ eṉak kūṟi, avaitammaic coṟṟoṭarkaḷil iyaittuk kūṟuṅkāl āṇpeyarkkēṟpa āṇ viṉaiyum peṇpeyarkkēṟpap peṇviṉaiyum kūṭṭi yuraikkaveṉa vitittāl ivai tammai aṟivuṭaiyōr kaṟka muntuvarā? vēṟu moḻi teritaṟku vaḻiyiṉṟi āriyattiṟ piṟantu iṭarppaṭuvārē ataṉaik kaṟṟaṟku uriyār. poruḷ vakaiyāl ītu uyartiṇai, ītu aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟu vakuttāl poruṭkēṟpac coṟkaḷai vaḻaṅkutalil yārkkum iṭarppāṭu tōṉṟātu. poruḷ vakaiyāl aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟē ulakattiṟku oppamuṭinta oru collai orukāl āṇākavum orukāṟ peṇṇākavum orukāl aliyākavum kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉa orumuṟaiyumiṉṟi vitittāl, ic coṟkaḷai ārāyntu ivai āṇ, ivai peṇ,ivai ali eṉa uṟuti ceytalilēyē māṇākkarkku vāṇāḷ kaḻiyum. iṉi, ivaṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭum poruḷai yuṇarntu ataṉai urimai kōṭaṟku vāṇāḷ eṅkē uḷatu? cila vāḻnāṭ palpiṇic ciṟṟaṟiviṉarāṉa makkaḷ tammuyirk kuṟutipayakkum uṇmaip poruḷai uṇarvataṟku oru karuviyāyamainta moḻi kaṟṟaṟkum payaṉpaṭuttaṟkum eḷitāka iruttal vēṇṭumē yallatu, kaṭiṉa colluṭaittātal payaṉiṉṟām. karuviyē peritiṭar payappatāyiṉ ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭu poruḷ payaṉtaruva tiyāṇṭumillai yeṉka. ittuṇaik kaṭiṉac cevvi yuṭaimaiyiṉālaṉṟō vaṭamoḻi ulaka vaḻakkiṉṟi iṟantoḻintatu. tiruñāṉacam pantap perumāṉ mutalāṉa aruṭṭiruvāḷarum muḻu mutaṟkaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭuy taṟkuriya eḷitāṉa neṟiyaic centamiḻt tiruppāṭṭukkaḷilē aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. ippeṟṟi iṉitu tērntē,
 kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
  paṇṇu ṟatterintāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
  maṇṇi ṭaiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
  eṇṇi ṭaippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō.
eṉṟu tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇa muṭaiyāruṅ kūṟiṉār.
centamiḻ naṇparkāḷ! iṟṟaikku nālāyirattu munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻmakkaḷum tamiḻ ilakkaṇa āciriyarum uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaip pākupāṭu poruḷ vakaiyāl iyaṟkaineṟi tiṟampātē uṇarntu aṟivuṭaiyār ellārkkum oppa muṭivatāka viḷakkiya ariya nuṇṇaṟivāṟṟal nuṅkaḷāṟ peritum pōṟṟaṟpālatēyām. iṉṉoraṉṉa vaṟṟai itukāṟum potuvāka niṉaittu vantatu pōl niṉaiyaṉmiṉ! paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa ovvoru poruḷum kiṭaittaṟkariya muḻumāṇikkaṅkaḷāmeṉṟu tērmiṉkaḷ! ivvarum peṟal māṇikkaṅkaḷellām varaṉmuṟaiyē tokutta tolkāppiyam vilai varampu aṟiyāk kaḷañciyam eṉṟu ārāyntu viyappeytumiṉkaḷ!
iṉi, nīr vēṭṭuc celkiṉṟāṉ oruvaṉ naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻum iḷamarakkāviṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē taṅki ayarvu ciṟitu oḻintu muppaḻac cāṟun tēṉum viraciṉāṟpōṟ cuvaikkum teṇṇīr nirampiya nīr nilaiyiṟ ceṉṟu nīr ārapparuki vēṭkaiyoḻintu ayarvu muṟṟum nīṅkiṉāṟpōla, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār tiruvāymalarntaruḷiya eḻuttati kāra collatikāraṅkaḷiṉiṭaiyē taṅkit tamiḻpperumai teriyā aṟiyāmai ciṟitu ciṟitē oḻintu ippōtu poruḷatikārattiṟ ceṉṟu āṇṭuvirinta nuṇporuṭ ṭokutiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu nukarntu emataṟiyāmai muṟṟum pōkkuvām. naṇparkāḷ! āciriyar poruḷai akam, puṟam eṉap pakutta muṟaiyē peritum nuṇṇaṟivu niṟaintatām. uyirkaḷiṉ aṟivu nikaḻcciyē akam, puṟam eṉa irutiṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uyiriṉuḷ nikaḻum aṟivu avvuyirkkē pulaṉāvatām. uyirkku ōr uṭal paṭaikkappaṭā muṉṉellām ataṉ aṟivu pulappaṭṭu nikaḻātāyc ceyaliṉṟi maḻuṅkik kiṭantatu. iṉi avvaṟivu aṅṅaṉam maḻuṅkikkiṭakka voṭṭātu ataṉai eḻuppi virivu ceytaṟporuṭṭu ataṟkōr uṭaltarappaṭṭatākaliṉ, uyir uḷḷaṟivu puṟapporuḷōṭu iyaintu niṉṟu ammuṟaiyē perukutaṟku ivvuṭampu iṭainiṉ ṟutavuvatoṉṟām. itaṉāl, akam, naṭu, puṟam eṉṉum mūvakaip peṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. akam aṟivu, naṭu uṭampu, puṟam poruḷ eṉa ākum. kaṇ oruporuḷaik kāṇṭaṟku ñāyiṟu viḷakkamāy iṭainiṉṟu kāṭṭutalpōla uyiraṟivu puṟapporuḷaip paṟṟutaṟku uṭampu oru viḷakkāmeṉka. itupaṟṟiyē meykaṇṭatēvar māyā taṉuviḷakkā maṟṟuḷḷaṅ kāṇātēl, āyātā moṉṟai eṉṟaruḷic ceytār. ivvulakiluḷḷa ellāp poruṭkuṇamum nuṭpavaṭivamāy ivvuṭampiṟ poruntik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟōṭu vēṟaṟa oṟṟumaippaṭṭu niṟkiṉṟa uyiraṟivu avaṟṟāl iṉpa tuṉpa nukarntu aṟiyāmai nīṅki viḷakkamuṭaiyatākiṉṟatu. veḷiyē tōṉṟum ellāp poruṭ kuṇaṅkaḷum ivvuṭaleṉṉum paḷikkup pāṟaiyilē veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavākaliṉ, itaṉ naṭuvil vāḻkiṉṟa uyiraṟivu ivaiyaṉaittaiyum uṇara vallatākiṉṟatu. ākavē akattē tōṉṟum uyiriṉ aṟivoḻukkaṅkaḷellām, puṟattē avaṟṟai eḻupputaṟkuk kāraṇamāyuḷḷa poruṭ pāku pāṭṭuṭaṉ poruntiyē irukkum. innuṭpa mellām muṟṟavum aṟinta āciriyar akavoḻukkattaik kaikkiḷai mutaṟ peruntiṇai iṟutiyāka eḻu kūṟākap pirittār; aṅṅaṉam pirittataṟkēṟpavē avaṟṟōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiya puṟapporuḷaiyum eḻu kūṟākkiṉār.
iṉi ōruyir piṟitōr uyirōṭu poruntum vaḻip piṟakkum iṉpatuṉpa nukarcciyellām āciriyar oruṅkē viḷakkiyuraikkum tiṟam mikap peritu. ivarpōl uyir iyaṟkai muḻutuṇarntu uraitta vēṟu pulavar āṅkilamoḻi valla cekappiriyar oruvarēyām. akattē tōṉṟum avviṉpa tuṉpa nikaḻccik kēṟṟa iṭam poḻutu mutalāka āciriyarāṉ ārāynturaikkap paṭṭaṉavellām iṉṟu kāṟum ulaka iyaṟkai piḻaiyātu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ulaka iyaṟkai muḻutuṇarn tilakkaṇa muraitta āciriyarai vēṟu ettēyattiṉum enta moḻiyiṉuṅ kāṇṭal aritu aritu.
iṉi, uyiriṉ akattē tōṉṟum uṇarvu nikaḻccika ḷellām ivvuṭampiṉ puṟattē mukattiṟ ṟōṉṟuṉpatu meyppāṭṭiyalil varampu ceyturaikkappaṭṭatu. iṉṉum ammukattiṟ kaṇkaḷilēyē aka nikaḻcci muḻutu muṇarappaṭumeṉṟu viḷakki,
nāṭṭa miraṇṭu maṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
  kūṭṭi yuraikkuṅ kuṟippurai yākum.
eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷai yiṭṭaruḷiṉār. teyvappulamait tiruvaḷḷuvarum itaṉaic ciṟappittuk kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. urimai naṇparkāḷ! ulaka iyaṟkai uyir iyaṟkai kaḷellām iṅṅaṉam varaiceyta ilakkaṇameḻutiya āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārai otta āciriyaraiyāyiṉum, allātavar aruḷic ceyta tolkāppiyam pōṉṟa piṟitoru nūlaiyāyiṉum iṟṟaikku nālāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ nākarikameytiya ettēcattilāyiṉum emmoḻiyi lāyiṉuṅ kāṭṭutal kūṭumā? naṭunilaitiṟampā turaimiṉkaḷ!
iṉi, ivvulakattiṉ uḷḷum puṟampumāy niṉṟu aṟiyavallatai aṟivittum, acaiya vallatai acaivittum pēroḷip piḻampāy aruvāy aṟivāy iṉpamāy viḷaṅkum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ nilaiyaic cuṭṭi āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iṟṟaikku nālāyira āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē niṟuviya poruṭṭiṭpattai atēkālattiṟ ṟōṉṟiya āriya cātiyār nūlkaḷiṟ colliya teyva nilaiyōṭu oppiṭṭuk kāṇpōmāyiṉ tamiḻaratu paṇṭaikkāla nākarika vaḷam mēlum mikkuviḷaṅkum.
āciriyar koṭi nilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa cūttirattil kantaḻi eṉṉum muḻumutaṟ paramporuḷ oru paṟṟumaṟṟu aruvāyta tāṉē niṟkun tattuvaṅ kaṭanta poruḷ eṉṟu valiyuṟuttār. iḵtataṟkurai kaṇṭa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟumāṟṟāṉ naṉku peṟappaṭum. itaṉāṟ kantaḻi eṉappaṭuṅ kaṭavuḷ cittu, caṭam eṉṉum iruvakai yulakiṉuḷḷum oṉṟaṉṟāy ivai tammaiyellām muṟṟum cūḻntu koṇṭu evvakaippaṟṟumiṉṟi nilaipeṟum pēriṉpa nilaiyameṉpatu peṟṟām. attuṇaip paḻaiya kālattē kaṭavuṭṭaṉmaiyiṉai ivvāṟuṇarn turaittavar ulakil vēṟu yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappaṭār. mikavum paḻaiya nūlāṉa irukkuvētattai ārāyntu pārmiṉ! irukkuvēta kālattu āriyar oḷiyuṭaip poruḷaiyum avaṟṟōṭu toṭar puṭaiyavaṟṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. itaṟkut tēva eṉṉuñ collē cāṉṟām; iccol oḷi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tiv eṉṉum vērpporu ḷiṉaṭiyākap piṟantatākum. ākavē, oḷiyuṟṟu viḷaṅkum viṇṇaiyum, vāṉmīṉkaḷaiyum, katiravaṉaiyum, viṭiyaṟ kālattaiyum, naṇpakaṟ poḻutaiyum, piṟavaṟṟaiyun teyvaṅ kaḷākat tuṇintu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. iṉṉun tēvar kaḷellām vāṉ. nilaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ  piḷḷai kaḷeṉṟum āṇṭāṇṭu an nūliṟ collappaṭṭaṉar. cōma eṉṉum oru vakaip pūṇṭai nacukkip piḻinteṭutta iracam matukkaḷippait taruvateṉavum; ic cōmaracamē vāṉ, nilaṉ, neruppu, katiravaṉ, intiraṉ, tirumāl eṉṉun teyvaṅkaḷai yellān tōṟṟuvitta teṉavum oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil irukku vētaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷil cāvittiriyum, neruppum tēvarkaṭku iṟappillā varam aruḷic ceytaṉar eṉkiṉṟatu. maṟṟuñ cila iṭaṅkaḷil ellāt tēvarkaḷum orē vakaiyāṉa āṟṟaluṭaiyareṉ ṟuraittup pōyp piṉ neruppu, intiraṉ, katiravaṉ eṉum mūvarē maṟṟai ellāriṉuñ ciṟantōrāva reṉkiṉṟatu. iṉṉum ivarait tavirat taya, pirutivi, atiti, ātityar, varuṇaṉ, parjaṉyaṉ, vāyu, maruttukkaḷ, mittiraṉ, pūṣaṉ, uṣa, acuviṉikaḷ, tvatiri, ripukkaḷ, vicuvakarmaṉ, prajāpati, prukapati, vāk, cōma, rutra, yamaṉ vicuva tēvarkaḷ, pitrukkaḷ mutalākat tēvar palappalar kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. viṣṇu eṉṟu collappaṭṭa teyvam tirumāl aṉṟu. cūriyaṉē kālai, naṇpakal, piṟpakal eṉṉu muppoḻutiṉum vāṉ kaṭantu cellu nilaiyil iṅkē viṣṇu veṉṉum peyarōṭu polikiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟata. uruttiraṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa teyvam civapirāṉ aṉṟu; cūṟāvaḷikkut teyvamē uruttiraṉ eṉṟu āṇṭuc collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. rutraṉ eṉṉuñ collukku vērpporuḷ muḻakkamiṭu vōṉ ūḷaiyiṭuvōṉ eṉpaṉavām. ivvēṟṟumai teriyātār uruttiraṉuñ civaṉum oruvarēyeṉat tamakkuttōṉṟiyavāṟē moḻivar. civam eṉpatu kantaḻi eṉṉum vēṟu peyarumuṭaiya paramporuḷ; aḵtu uruttiraṉ piramaṉ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattut tēvarkaṭkellām appāṟpaṭṭatoṉṟām; ivvuṇmai,
tēvarkō vaṟiyātatēva tēvaṉ ceḻumpoḻilkaḷ 
  	payantukāt taḻikku maṟṟai
  mūvarkkō ṉāyniṉṟa mutalvaṉ.
eṉṟa māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ arumait tiruvākkāṉum naṉṟu teruṭṭappaṭum. irukku vētam muḻuvatum turuvit turuvip pārppiṉum civam eṉṉum paramporuḷiyalpu kāṇappaṭamāṭṭātu. aṅkellām ulaka iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷāṟ ṟāmpeṟum payaṉ kuṟittu avai tammaiyellām tam aṟiyāmaiyāṟ ṟeyvaṅkaḷākak karuti āriyamakkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vanta kīḻnilai aṟivu pulappaṭak kiṭakkumē yallatu, paramporuṭ corūpa muṇarnta mēlnilai aṟivu ciṟitāyiṉum āṇṭup peṟappaṭutalillai. aṟiviṟ ciṟanta tamiḻ makkāḷ! iṅṅaṉam vētattiṉ uṇmaikūṟi māntarkku aṟivu koḷutta varuvāraik kaṇṭu maṉam puḻuṅki uyar kulattiṉareṉat tammai iṟumān teṇṇi yiruppār cilar avai tikar eṉap puṟampaḻippar. avar kūṟum ap puṟam paḻippuraikkuc ciṟitum añcaṉmiṉ! evar yātu kūṟiṉum uṇmai karakkappaṭāteṉka. ittuṇaik kīḻnilai aṟivaiyē pōtittup payaṉiṉṟi virintukiṭakkum avvētattaip piramāṇamākak koḷḷātavar eṉṟu emmaip puṟam paḻippatupaṟṟi nammaṉōrkku varum iḻukkeṉṉai? oruciṟitu millaiyākaliṉ, nām tattuva vurimai kaiviṭāmāy meypporuḷ kūṟutalaiyē kaṭaip piṭippāmāka. namakkum maṟṟum aṟivuṭaiyār ellārkkuñ ciṟanta piramāṇa meynnūlkaḷāyuḷḷa tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyaṉavē namakkuc cālumeṉṟu maṉantiruntu miṉkaḷ! piṟar kūṟum pakaṭṭuraikkañci uṇmaiyaik kaiviṭṭu viṭātīrkaḷ!
iṉic, civam eṉṉum ap paramporuḷnilai avvirukkuvētattiṟ kūṟappaṭātatu koṇṭu ataṉ perumai kuṉṟumāṟillai. parappiramapporuḷ nilaiyaṟiya māṭṭāta aṟivillāp piḷḷaimaip pakkuvattiṉ kaṇṇarāṉa āriyamakkaḷ ceyta av virukku vētattiṟ kaḻiperunuṇṇaṟivāṟ ṟamiḻnaṉmakkaḷaṟinta ammuḻumutaṟ poruḷ nilai evvāṟu kāṇappaṭum? ciṟār iḻaittu viḷaiyāṭum maṇaṟciṟṟililē nuṇṇaṟivuvāytta taccariyaṟṟum aḻakiya māṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭataluṇṭō? enta nuṇporuḷ evar vayiṉ varumō aḵtu avarpālē ārāyaṟpālatām. aḵtoḻintu ōr aṟivuṭaiyāriṭattup peṟappaṭum ōrarum poruḷai ēṉaiyōr aṟivilāriṭattuk kēṭka muntuvāraippōl tamiḻ nūṟkaḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭum antaraṅka meypporuṭkup piramāṇam vaṭanūliṟ ṟēṭutal peritum nakaiyāṭi ikaḻaṟkē ētuvām. ivvāṟṟāl yām vaṭanūlkaḷai ikaḻkiṉṟēm eṉṟu niṉaiyaṉmiṉ! yām avaṟṟiṉ uṇmaittaṉmai kūṟiyatē yaṉṟip  piṟitillai. iṉi vaṭanūliṟ ciṟanta nuṇṇaṟivu kāṇappaṭum cāṅkiyam, upaniṭatam, vētānta cūttiram, civākamam mutaliyavaṟṟai iṅṅaṉan tāḻttuk kūṟutaṟku yām oru ciṟitum oruppaṭēm oruppaṭēm.
iṉi avvirukkuvētattiṟ pōnta ap paltēvar vaḻipāṭēṉum viḻuppamuṭaiyavā veṉṟu oruciṟitu ārāyvām. matukkaḷippai viḷaittu aṟivai mayakki maḻukkum cōmaracapāṉattaic ciṟappittup pukaḻnta patikam oṉṟaṉai iṅkē moḻi peyarttu varaikiṉṟōm; atu koṇṭu avvirukku vētappāṉmai aṟivuṭaiyārkku ellām iṉitu viḷaṅkakkiṭakkum.
irukkuvēta oṉpatām maṇṭilam,
mutaṟpatikam cōmapavamāṉam

intiraṉ parukutaṟporuṭṭu naṟukkippiḻinta
ōcōmaracamē! mikat tittippatāy makiḻnta celaviṉai
yuṭaiya nīrōṭaipōl cuttamāy niṉvaḻiyē oḻukuka.	(1)
pakaivarai yaṭakkuvōy! makkaḷellārkkum naṭpāḷa!
apparicaiyāl avaṉtaṉ uyarnilaiyaiyum irupparaṇaiyum
aṭaintāṉ.	(2)
nī vīruttiraṉaik kōṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭaiyai yākuka,
iṉpan tarutaṟkut talaimaiyuṭaiyai yākuka, mikunta
vaḷḷaṉmai yuṭaiyai yākuka: celvamikunta em
iḷavaracar koṭaiyai virutticeyka.	(3)
valiya tēvarkaṭku āṟṟum viruntiṟku niṉatu iracat
tai  oḻukacceyka: eṅkaṭku valivum pukaḻum uṇ
ṭātaṟporuṭṭu iṅkē varuka.	(4)
ō intuvē! nāḷunāḷum ivvāṟē karuttōṭu
niṉṉarukē varukiṉṟōm: niṉakkē em vēṇṭukōḷ
mantiraṅkaḷ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.	(5)
cūriyaṉ putalvi eṉṟum yāṭṭumayirk kampalattāl
niṉ cōmaracattai vaṭittuca cuttiyākki oḻukaviṭukiṉṟāḷ.	(6)
melliyarāṉa patiṉmartōḻimār naṟukkum iyantirattil 
avaṉaip piṭittuvaittu muṭivunāḷ varaiyil avaṉai viṭātu 
koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.	(7)
akkaṉṉimakaḷir piṉ avaṉaip pōkaviṭukiṉṟār: icai
kūṭṭuvāṉaippōl avarkaḷ turutti ūtip
pakaivarai oṭṭum mukkūṭṭup pāṉattai
urukac ceykiṉṟaṉar.	(8)
intiraṉ parukutaṟporuṭṭu, ūṟuceyyappaṭāta pāl
ākkaḷ avaṉaic cuṟṟiluṅ kūṭiyiruntu putiya iḷañ
cōmaracattil tamatupālaik kalappikkiṉṟaṉa.	(9)
ivviracattaip parukiṉa perukaḷippāl intiraṉ
viruttirarai ellām kolkiṉṟāṉ: avaṉ taṉcelvat
tai namakkuc corintu tarukiṉṟāṉ.	(10)
ippeṟṟiyavām patikaṅkaḷē irukkuvētam muḻuvatum nirampik kiṭaṭakkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉōraṉṉa patikaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa vaḻipāṭukaḷ iṉṟum mika manta pakkuvamuṭaiya mākkaḷ nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṭṭēri, iruḷaṉ, kaṟuppaṉ mutaliya ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷai vaḻipaṭṭu varum muṟaikaḷōṭu ottiruk kiṉṟaṉavē yallatu vēṟeṉṉai? iṉṉum āriyar cōmaracapāṉattāṉ vēḷvikaḷ vēṭṭataṉṟiyum veḷḷāṭukaḷ, cemmaṟiyāṭakaḷ, pacumāṭukaḷ, erumai māṭukaḷ, kutiraikaḷ, maṉitar mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷaik koṉṟuvēṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉaip pucittu vantārkaḷ. maṉitaṉ maṉitaṉaik koṉṟu pucikkuñ ceykai evvaḷavu koṭumaiyāṉa ceyal! nakkavāram mutaliya tīvakaṅkaḷil vacikkum mikka anākarika cātiyārē iṉṟum maṉita ūṉ pucittu varukiṉṟaṉar. uyirkaḷil maṉitarkkup palavakaiyālum payaṉpaṭutaluṭaiya pacumāṭu, kutirai mutaliyavaṟṟaic ciṟitum irakkamiṉṟik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉaivirumpi vēṇṭiyamaṭṭum pucittu vantaṉarāyiṉ, paṇṭaik kāla āriyacātiyār evvaḷavu koṭiyavarka ḷeṉṟum evvaḷavu aṟiyāmai yuṭaiyavarkaḷeṉṟum eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu! ivvaḷavu koṭuñ ceyaluñ ceytu koṇṭu, ikkoṭuñceyaluk kutaviyākat tām pāṭi vaittuk koṇṭa iḻinta vētappāṭṭukkaiḷait tammiṉ vēṟāṉa tamiḻmakkaḷum oruppaṭṭut taḻuvic ciṟappikkumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttu varum avar ceyal niṉaiyuṅkā lellāñ cālavum vañcaṉaiyuṭaitteṉṟu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ittakaiya koṭuñ ceykaiyuñ cuvarkkattait tarumeṉṟa avaratu pōlip poyyuraiyai maṟuttaṟkaṉṟē nammutu kuravarāṉa teyvappulamait tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
avicorin tāyiram vēṭṭaliṉ oṉṟaṉ
  uyircekut tuṇṇāmai naṉṟu.
eṉṟu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiṉār. cāṅkiyam eṉṉum ariya tattuvanūl vakutteḻutiya pēraṟivāḷarāṉa kapilarum kaṇakkillāp pirāṇikaḷaik kolvittu avaṟṟiṉ irattattai āṟāyp perukac ceyvittaliṉ vētaṅkaḷ acuttamuṭaiyaṉa.1 eṉṟu ōtiṉār. atu niṟka.
iṉi, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu irukkuvētam mutaliyaṉa cāmāṉiya pautika vaḻipāṭṭil talai niṉṟaṉavāyiṉum, avvēta muṭipāṉa upaniṭataṅkaḷum cāṅkiyam vētānta cūttiram mutaliyaṉavumellām uyarnta tattuva nuṇporuḷ pōtikkak kāṇṭaliṉ āriyamakkaḷ piṟṟaiñāṉṟu pēraṟivuṭaiyarāyiṉā reṉṉāmō veṉiṉ;- eṉṉām. upaniṭata mutalāṉa ñāṉanūlkaḷ āriyamoḻiyil eḻutap paṭṭiruttalpaṟṟi avai āriyamakkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟal vaḻuvām.
paṇṭaināḷil tamiḻmakkaḷ vaṭakkē imayamalaic cāralvaraiyilum, mēṟkē āpkāṉitāṉam varaiyilum paraviyiruntaṉar. itaṟku aṟikuṟiyāka iṉṟum āpkāṉi tāṉam pelucitāṉam eṉṉum nāṭukaḷil tamiḻōṭu mikka toṭarpuṭaiya pirāki eṉṉum moḻi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. imayamalaic cāralil vāḻum kuṉṟavar kaḷellārum tamiḻiṉ ṟiripāṉa oru moḻi vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. ituvē yaṉṟi vaṭanāṭṭiṟ paḻaiya pala ūrkaḷellān tamiḻp peyarpeṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻūk eṉṉum ūr vaṭanāṭṭiliruttal yārumaṟinta toṉṟēyām. iññāṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭi liruppōriṟ perumpālār paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻ marapiṉarēyāvar. moḻi kālavēṟupāṭṭāl vēṟu paṭiṉum makkaḷ cātip piṟappu yāṇṭum orutaṉmaiyākavē yirukkum. muṉnāḷiṟ ṟamiḻar mikap pukaḻ peṟṟu vāḻntapōtu vaṭaāciyāviliruntu cintu natip pakkamāy āriyamakkaḷ intiyāviṉuṭ puka, uḷḷirunta tamiḻariyaṟkaiyilē viruntu varuvārai ōmpippōṟṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭaiyarātalāl tam nāṭu tēṭip piḻaikkavanta āriyamakkaḷai nalvaravāka ēṟṟu avarkku vēṇṭuvaṉa tantu ōmpiṉār. āriyar varuṅkālattut tamiḻar mikka nākarika vāḻvuṟṟiruntaṉa reṉpatai irukkuvēta vuraikoṇṭu muṉṉarē iṉitu viḷakkiṉām. iṉi, aṅṅaṉam pōnta āriya makkaḷōṭu tamiḻar peritum aḷavaḷāviṉārakaliṉ, avarkkuriya āriya moḻiyiṉaiyun tāṅkaṟṟu ataṉaiyum paṇpaṭuttuvantaṉar. āriyamakkaḷ ceyyum vēḷvi viḻākkaḷukkut tāmum poruḷutavi ceytu avaṟṟai naṭaipeṟuvittu vantaṉarāyiṉum, avvēḷvi vēṭṭal ciṟitum payaṉpaṭāmai aṟivittu avarkkut tattuva nuṇporuḷkaḷaiyun tamiḻar āṅkāṅku uṇarttu vārāyiṉar. putiyarāy vantōr tām vaḻakkamāka mēṟkoṇṭu ceyyum oru karumattaic caṭutiyiṟ ceyyavēṇṭāmeṉṟu niṟuttiṉāl avarmika maṉam varuntuvarākaliṉ, avarkku mutalil utaviyāḷarāy niṉṟu avar vēṇṭiya vēḷvik kaṉmaṅkaḷaiyum naṭāttivanta tamiḻar piṉ ciṟitu ciṟitē avarkkut tattuva nuṇporuḷaṟivu koḷutti vēḷvi vēṭṭal payaṉpaṭāmaiyē yaṉṟit tīviṉaiyuṭait tātaluṅ kāṭṭuvar. iṅṅaṉan tamiḻāciriyar kāṭṭiya aṟivumoḻikaḷē upaniṭataṅkaḷeṉṟu piṟṟaiñāṉṟu peyar peṟalāyiṉa. upaniṭataṅkaḷellām oru mukamāy niṉṟu vēḷvivēṭṭa lākāteṉṟum, makkaḷellārun tattuvañāṉan talaippaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. vētaṅkaḷiṟ colliya kaṉmaṅkaḷukku muṟṟum māṟupāṭāy niṉṟu avai tammaip paḻittut tattuva ñāṉa mēmpāṭu pulappaṭukkum peṟṟiyaṉavām. avai vētāntameṉavum peyarpeṟukiṉṟaṉa. vētamum vētāntamum tammiṟ peritum muraṇi niṟpaṉavām. vētam kaṉmaṅkaḷai mikkeṭuttuk kūṟuvaṉa; vētāntam avaṟṟaip paḻittu ñāṉattai mikkeṭuttuk kūṟuvaṉa; vētam āriya makkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; vētāntam tamiḻmakkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavām. vētamaṭṭum aṟinta āriyar vētāntamaṟinta tamiḻarpāl ataṉaik kaṟṟaṟivā rāyiṉar. paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻarkkuṭ pukaḻpeṟṟa vēntarā yirunta caṉakaṉ caivali, acātacatturu mutaliya maṉṉar maṉṉar āriyarkku vētāntam uṇarttiya varalāṟu catapātapirāmaṇam, upaniṭatam mutaliya nūlkaḷāṟ ṟeṟṟeṉa viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻamaṉṉarum ammaṉṉar kuṭiyārum āriyarāl araciṉiyār eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭavāṟu muṉ iṉitu viḷakkip pōntām. appeṟṟiyarām rājanyar āriyarkkut tattuvañāṉam pulaṅkoḷa aṟivuṟuttu vantamaikku īṇṭu iraṇṭōr utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutum:

cāntōkkiya upaniṭatam ru-vatu pirapāṭakam, 3-vatu kaṇṭam, cuvētakētu āruṇēyar pāñcāla maṉṉaṉ avaikkaḷattē ceṉṟār. piravākaṉa caivali eṉṉum avvēntaṉ avarai nōkkip piḷḷāy! Ã‹wªij Ãd¡F Phndhg njrŠ brŒjduh? eṉṟu kēṭṭāṉ. aiyā! ōm avar ceytaṉar eṉṟu avar viṭai kūṟiṉār. (1)
‘innilattiṉiṉṟum makkaḷ eṅku eḻukiṉṟaṉar nī aṟivaiyā?’ eṉṟu vēntaṉ viṉaviṉāṉ ataṟku avar ‘aiyā, nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār ‘tirumpa avar eṅku celkiṉṟār aṟivaiyā?’ eṉa viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟkavar ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉa iṟuttār; tēvayāṉattiṟkum, pitiryāṉat tiṟkum eṅkē pētamuṇṭākiṉṟatu aṟivaiyā?’ eṉa viṉaviṉāṉ; ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār.(2)
‘maṟumaiyulakam ēṉ niṟaikkap paṭuvatillai aṟivaiyā?’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟku avar ‘aṟiyēṉ aiyā’ eṉṟār; ‘pañcamākutiyil tiravapporuḷkaḷ ēṉ puruṣacaptameytukiṉṟaṉa aṟivaiyā?’ eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ; ataṟkavar ‘aiyā nāṉ aṟiyēṉ’ eṉṟār.(3)
appaṭiyāyiṉ upatēcam peṟṟēṉeṉṟu ēṉ iṅku vantāy? oṉṟumaṟiyātavaṉ aṟivikkappaṭṭāṉeṉṟu nī eppaṭic collalām? eṉṟāṉ aracaṉ. ataṉaik kēṭṭalum mikka vicaṉattōṭum taṉ tantaiyiṭam tirumpip pōy tantāy! eṉakkoṉṟum upatēciyāmalē, ellām upatēcikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟīrē’ eṉṟāṉ.(4)
appollāta aracaṉ eṉṉai aintu viṉākkaḷ viṉāviṉāṉ, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟkēṉum eṉṉāl viṭai yiṟukka muṭiyavillaiyē eṉṟāṉ. atu kēṭṭa tantai ‘nī coṉṉa ak kēḷvikaḷ aintaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟkēṉum eṉakkē viṭai teriyavillaiyē; avai eṉakkut terintaṉavāyiṉ niṉakkēṉ yāṉ collāmaliruppēṉ? eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ(5).
kautamakōttirattārāṉa avar tantai avvaracaṉ araṇmaṉaikkup pōyiṉār. avar varukaiyaik kaṇṭa aracaṉ viruntiṉarkkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭukaḷellām āṟṟiṉāṉ. maṟunāṭ kālaiyil avar aracaṉ avaik kaḷattē ceṉṟār. aracaṉ avarai nōkki, ō kautamarē!  ulakattup poruḷkaḷiṟ ciṟantatetuveṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟīrō ataṉaik kēḷum eṉṟāṉ. ataṟkavar ulakiyaṟporuḷkaḷ eṉakku vēṇṭām avai niṉpālē yirukkaṭṭum. ō vēntaṉē! nī eṉ putalvaṉaik kēṭṭa aintu viṉākkaḷaiyum eṉakku viḷakkic collutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār (6)
atukēṭṭaracaṉ vicaṉamuṭaiyāṉay, eṉṉuṭaṉ cilanāḷ irukkakkaṭavīr eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. atu kaḻintapiṉ aracaṉ avarai nōkki, nīr ivvāṟu vicāraṇai ceyyap pukuntamaiyāṉum, itaṟku muṉ pirāmaṇaṉ evaṉum ivvirakaciyattai aṟiyāṉākalāṉum, ivvulakattiluḷḷa ellā makkaḷuḷḷum rājanyarkku māttiramē itaṉai upatēcikkum urimaiyuṇṭākalāṉum yām itaṉai umakku aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟām eṉṟu upatēcikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭatu.
iṉṉum ivaipōla upaniṭataṅkaḷil āṇṭāṇṭu varuvaṉavellām īṇṭuk kāṭṭaluṟiṉ itu mikaviriyumeṉa vañci viṭukkiṉṟām. ivai tammāl tēṟappaṭum muṭipurai yāteṉiṉ, rājanyar allatu araciṉiyār eṉṟu āriyarāl aḻaikkappapeṟṟa paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē āriyap pirāmaṇarkkut tattuva ñāṉōpatēcañ ceytu vantaṉa reṉpatēyām eṉka. ākavē, upaniṭatam tamiḻāciriyarāl āriyamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa veṉṉumuṇmai teḷiporuḷāy nilainiṟuttap paṭṭatu. vētānta cūttiram, cāṅkiyam, naiyāyikam, vaicēṭikam, mutaliyaṉavellām vakuttu nuṇporuḷ potuḷa eḻutiṉār tamiḻāciriyarēyā meṉpatūum, puttacamayam viḷakkiya kautamacākkiyarum tamiḻāciriyarēyā meṉpatūum mullaippāṭṭārāycci, tirukkuṟaḷārāycciyil naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉām. āṇṭuk kāṇka. ivvāṟu tattuvañāṉaṅkaḷum avai tirunta viḷakkiya upaniṭatam civākama mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻarkkē uriyaṉavāyp piṉ āriyarāṟ ṟamiḻariṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭa āriya iravaṟporuṇūlkaḷāy nilaippavum, iññāṉṟai āriyar avai tamakkē uriyaṉaveṉavum, avai tammait tamiḻar ōtappeṟāreṉavuṅ kūṟutal ciṟumakārāṉum eḷḷi nakaiyāṭaṟpālatām. iññāṉṟai āriyar tamiḻar ceyta naṉṟiyaic ciṟitum ōrātu tīṭṭiṉa marattiṟ kūrpārttal oppat tamakku aṟivukoḷuttiya paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ marapiṉarām nammaṉōrpāṟ ceytupōtarum paṭiṟṟoḻukkam mikapperitu! avar paṭiṟṟoḻukka viyalpu iṉait teṉṟaṟiyāta nammaṉōrum avarmayakkavuraiyil viḻuntu avamēyākiṉṟār. iṉiyēṉum tam peruñciṟap puṇarntu ākkameytuvārāka.

piṉ iṇaippu
1. uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam

in nilaulakattil uyir uḷ poruḷkaḷum uyiril poruḷkaḷum ākiya iruvakaiyum oruṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, uyiruḷ poruḷkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭumukattāl uyiril poruḷkaḷ ārāyntaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉavēyaṉṟi, ivai muṉṉaiyavaṟṟiṉ toṭarpiṉṟi aṅṅaṉam aṟiyap paṭutalaik kāṇkilēm. ākavē, uyirp poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa aṟivu peṟṟāl, avvaṟivu peṟuvārk kellām aḵtu aṟivum iṉpamum aḷittu, avaratu maṇṇulaka vāḻkkaiyai viṇṇulaka vāḻkkai yōṭoppac ceyyun tiṟattatām eṉpataṉai aṉaivarum niṉaiviṟ patittal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam iṉṟiyamaiyātu ārāyntu aṟiyaṟ pālaṉavākiya uyirkaḷō eṇṇiṟanta kōṭiyaṉa; eṉṟālum, avaitammiṟ pulaṉākum uṇarviṉ aṟiviṉ perumai ciṟumai kaṇṭu, avaitammai āṟu tokutikaḷākap pakuttup, paṇṭait tamiḻāciriyarākiya tolkāppiyaṉār aḷavu paṭutti yirukkiṉṟār. toṭṭāluṇarum uṇarccimaṭṭum uṭaiya ōr aṟivuyirkaḷum, toṭutaluṭaṉ nāviṉāṟ cuvaittuṇarum īr aṟivuyirkaḷum, toṭutal cuvaittaluṭaṉ mōntuṇarum mūkkuṇarvum vāynta mūvaṟivuyirkaḷum, im mūṉṟaṉuṭaṉ kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭuṇaruṅ kaṇṇuṇarvum vāynta nālaṟivuyirkaḷum, innāṉkaṉuṭaṉ ceviyāṟ kēṭṭuṇaruñ ceviyuṇarvum vāynta aiyaṟivuyirkaḷum, iṉi ivvavaim pulaṉkaḷāl aṟintavaṟṟai, nallatitu tīyatitu, iḵtu itaṉaiyoppatu, iḵtu itaṉiṉ vēṟāvatu eṉappakuttuk kāṇum maṉavaṟivum oruṅkukeḻumiya āṟaṟivuyirkaḷum, eṉa āṟu perumpakuppil ellā uyirkaḷaiyum aṭakki, avaṟṟiṟku ovvoru pakuppiliruntum orucila uyirkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṉ aṟiviyakkattaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉaṟār āciriyar.1 ivvāṟāka uyirkaḷellāvaṟṟaiyum, avvavaṟṟiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum aṟiviṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu kaṇṭu, āṟu vakaiyil aṭakkikkāṭṭiya mikap paḻaiya nūl tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa tolkāppiyattait tavira, vēṟentap paḻaiya moḻiyilum entap paḻaiya nūlum illāmai karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. itukoṇṭu, paḻaiya tamiḻāciriyariṉ nuṭpa ārāycciyaṟiviṉ māṭci teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam pakukkappaṭṭa aṟuvakai uyirkaḷil, maṉavaṟivuṭaṉ āṟaṟivukūṭiya makkaḷē uyarnta piṟappiṉar. ataṉāl, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār makkaḷai uyartiṇai eṉṟum, makkaḷ allāta ēṉai aintaṟivukkuṭpaṭṭa uyirkaḷai aḵṟiṇai eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉār.2
iṉit, toṭutaluṇarcci oṉṟēyuṭaiya pullum maraṉumē mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya uyirkaḷākum. innilavulakattiṉ mēṟparappellāṅ kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākap paṉippāṟaikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ap paṉippāṟaikaḷ pakalavaṉ veppattāl urukik karaiyak karaiya, nīrperukip paḷḷattākkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil nirampikkoṇṭu parampavē, āṟum ēriyum ciṟukaṭal peruṅkaṭa lkaḷumākiya nīrnilaikaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa.
piṉṉar, in nīrnilaikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ōraṟivuyir kaḷiṟ kaṭaippaṭi yiṉavākiya nīrppūṇṭukaḷum, īraṟivuyir kaḷākiya nattai, caṅku, ippi, kiḷiñcil mutaliyaṉavum, mūvaṟiviṉatākiya aṭṭaiyum, nāṉ kaṟiviṉatākiya naṇṭum, aiyaṟiviṉavākiya mīṉkaḷum piṟ piṟkālaṅkaḷiṟ ṟōṉṟuva vāyiṉa.
nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya nirppūṇṭukaḷ kālañ cellac cella nilattiṉmēl ivarntu vaḷara, avaṟṟaiyaṭuttu vāṉaḷāviya pariya pariya maraṅkaḷ tōṉṟalum, avai aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ pala āṅkāṅku immaṇmicaip polivavāyiṉa. ittakaiya kāṭukaḷiṟ pala pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukkuppiṉ maṭintu nilattiṉkaṭ putaintu pōkavē, paṭṭuppōṉa avaikaḷiṉ maraṅkaḷ pala nūṟṇṭukaḷukkup piṉ karikaḷāka urumāṟiṉa; aṅṅaṉam māṟiya karikaḷē, ippōtu nilattai akaḻnta curaṅkaṅkaḷiliruntum mikutiyāy eṭukkappaṭṭu, nīrāvi vaṇṭikaḷaiyum, nīrāvik kappalkaḷaiyum viraivākac celuttutaṟkup peritum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, nilattiṉkaṇ iyaṅkum uyirkaḷil iruppaṉa, ūrvaṉa, naṭappaṉa, paṟappaṉa, nīntuvaṉavākiya ellā  uyirkaḷum mutaṉmutal nīriṉ kaṭṭōṉṟip piṟkālaṅ kaḷiṟṟām nilattiṉmēl ēṟivantu uyirvāḻappeṟṟaṉa. itaṟkuc cāṉṟāka nīrkkōḻiyaiyum nīrāmaiyaiyum uṟṟup pārmiṉkaḷ! nīrkkōḻi nīruḷ amiḻnti vāḻavum vallatu, nilattiṉ mēl iyaṅkavum vallatu, vāṉiṟ paṟantu cellavum vallatu. maṟṟu, nīrāmai nīril nīnti uyirkāvaḻtaṟkut tuṇaiceyyum ceṭṭaikaḷ vāyntatāyiruttal pōlavē, nila āmai nilattiṉmēl naṭamāṭutaṟ kēṟṟa aṭikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya kālkaḷ vāyttiruttal aṟiyaṟpālatu. iṉṉum nilattiṉkaṇ ulavum nāy, kutirai, yāṉa, kuraṅku mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷō ṭoppa, nīrnāy, nīrkkutirai, nīryāṉai, nīrkkuraṅku, mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷ kaṭaliṉkaṇ ulavā niṟṟalaik, kaṇkāṭci nilaiyaṅkaḷil avaitammiṟ cila koṇarntu vaikkappaṭṭiruttalaik kaṇṭu naṉkaṟiyalām.
iṉṉum, nilattiṉmēl uḷḷa uyirkaḷaik kāṭṭilum ettaṉaiyō maṭaṅku periya uruvam vāynta uyirkaḷ, makkaḷ tōṉṟāta annāḷiṟ kaṭal nīrilum maṇalveḷikaḷilum iyaṅkiṉa. makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉṉāḷilum avviraṇṭiluṅ kāṇappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.1 nilattiṉmēl uḷḷa tēḷai viṭa mika mikap periya uruviṉavākiya kaṭaṟṟēḷkaḷ oṉpataṭi nīḷam uḷḷaṉavāy annāḷil tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. aṅṅaṉamē, mikap pariyavāṉa cila vilaṅkukaḷ eṇpattu nāṉkaṭi nīḷamum, maṟṟuñ cila nūṟaṭi nīḷamum uḷḷaṉavāyiruntaṉa. iññāṉṟuḷḷa yāṉaiyaiviṭap pattup patiṉaintumaṭaṅku periya uruviṉavāṉa pēriyāṉaikaḷum aññāṉṟiruntaṉa. āṉālum, ivvilaṅkukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa makkaḷ tōṉṟu taṟku muṉṉamē māṇṭaṭoḻintaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, uyirmāṇṭu paṉippāṟaikaḷiṉ ūṭē kiṭantu putaintupōṉavai piṉnāḷil ap paṉippāṟaikaḷ katiravaṉ veppattāl urukik karaintuviṭavē, ettaṉaiyō āyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ māṇṭu pōṉa avaṟṟiṉ muḻuvuṭampum puttap putiyaṉavāyk kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. paṉippāṟaikaḷ illāta iṭaṅkaḷil māyntavaikaḷō aṅkuḷḷa maṇ kaṟpāṟaikaḷāy iṟukiviṭa avaṟṟiṉiṭaiyē kiṭantu tacai kaḻintu maṭkippōka, veṟum eṟpuk kūṭukaḷāy veṭṭi veḷiyē eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam, iruppaṉa, ūrvaṉa, naṭappaṉa, paṟappaṉa, nīntuvaṉavākiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ mutaṉmutal nīriṟ ṟōṉṟi iyaṅkip, piṉṉar avaṟṟiṟ pala nilattiṉ mēl ivarntu, varavarap palki vāḻalāyiṉāṟ pōlavē, makkaḷ yākkai yiṉaiyuṭaiya uyirkaḷum mutalil nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟip, piṉṉar nilattiṉ mēl vantu uyirvāḻntu perukalā yiṉamaiyum oru cila nikaḻccikaḷāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. oru kāl mēlnāṭṭu makkaḷāṟ celuttappaṭṭuc ceṉṟa oru marakkalam oru kaṭaṟpakutiyiṟ celluṅkāl, aṅkuḷḷa kaṭalnīriṉiṉṟum makkaḷ vaṭiviṉatākiya ōr āṇ uyir, am marakkalattiṉmēl ēṟivantu, ataṉ muṉ aṇiyattil niṟuttappaṭṭirunta oru peṇpāvai yuruviṉaic ciṟitunēram uṟṟunōkkik koṇṭiruntu, akkappal mīkāmaṉum piṟarun taṉṉaip piṭikka muyaṉṟapōtu, akkaṭalnīriṟ kutittu amiḻntippōṉa uṇmai varalāṟṟiṉai ematu `ciṟuvarkkāṉa centamiḻ eṉṉum nūlil eṭuttukkāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēm. ittakaiya nikaḻcciyiliruntu, kaṭalnīrilum makkaḷ vaṭiviṉavākiya uyirkaḷ iyaṅkutal aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? ivvāṟu ellā uyirkaḷum ivvūṉ uṭampiṟ ṟēṉṟutaṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta iṭamākiya kaṭalnīril mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiyē, paṉṉeṭuṅ kālaṅkaḷukkup piṟaku makkaḷum nīrvāḻkkaiyai viṭṭu nilavāḻkkaiyaik kaṭaippiṭikkalā yiṉareṉṟu uṇartal vēṇṭum.
aḵtuṇmaiyēyāyiṉum, uyirvāḻkkaikku iṉṟi yamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum ellāk karuvikaḷum oruṅku vāykkappeṟṟa innilattiṉmicai uyirkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟāmal, nīriṉkaṇ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟalāṉatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṟ, kūṟutum: paṭaippiṉ tuvakkattilēyē im maṇṇulakam uyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaikku ēṟṟatāy iruntāl, puṉmutal makkaḷ īṟāṉa ellā uyirkaḷum im maṭpāṅkar micaiyē tōṉṟiyirukkum. āṉāl, innilavulakiṉ iyalpō pallāyiraṅkōṭi āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ irunta nilaivēṟu, iṅñāṉṟuḷḷa nilai muḻuvatūum vēṟu.
veyyavaṉ maṇṭaṭilam oru perunerupput tiraḷaiyāy aṉṟum iṉṟum viḷaṅkāniṟkiṉṟatu. itu niṉaittaṟkum iyalāk kaṭuviraivuṭaṉ cuḻalā niṟkaiyil innilamum, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, caṉi mutalāṉa kōḷkaḷum avveyyavaṉākiya tīppiḻampiṉiṉṟun teṟittuppōnta neruppup poṟikaḷēyākum. ciṟiya poruḷkaḷaiyē aṟintu nukar taṟpāṉmaiyavāṉa kaṇ mutalāṉa ciṟupoṟikaḷ maṭṭumē amaiyappeṟṟa ciṟṟaṟiviṉarākiya nammaṉōr kaṇkaḷukku nām vāḻum immaṇṇulakam mikap periyatupōṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, uṇmaiyāṉ ārāyntu kāṇpārkkuk katiravaṉ maṇṭilamē innilavulakattaip pārkkilum munnūṟṟu muppatiṉāyira maṭaṅku periyatātal viḷaṅkumeṉṟu iññāṉṟai vāṉnūlāciriyar kaṇakkuc ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar.1 ataṉāl, immaṇṇulakaṅ katiravaṉ maṇṭilattiṉiṉṟun teṟittup pōnta oru ciṟu neruppup poṟiyēyallāl vēṟaṉṟātal tērntuṇaraṟpāṟṟu. aṅṅaṉam oru neruppuppoṟiyāy mutaṉ mutal uṇṭāṉa innilavulaku ettaṉaiyō nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷāy vemmai kuṟaintu āṟi iṟukivarukiṉṟatu. iṉṟuṅkūṭa itaṉ uṭpakuti aḷaviṭappaṭāta veppam vāynta tīkkuḻampu uṭaiyatāyk kotittuk koṇṭiruk kiṉṟatu. innilattiṉ maiyamāṉa iṭattiṉūṟu āṅkāṅkuḷḷa erimalaikaḷiṉ puḻaivaḻiyē ittīkkuḻampu orōvorukāṟ kotittup poṅki mēleḻuntu vaḻintuviṭutal uṇṭu; ivvāṟu vaḻitalāl erimalaikaḷiṉ aṭivārattilum aṇmaiyilum uḷḷa ūrkaḷum nakarkaḷum erintu cāmpalāyviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi 501 - ām āṇṭil vecuviyacu eṉṉum erimalaiyiṉiṉṟum poṅki vaḻinta tīkkuḻampāl ataṉai aṭuttirunta periya nakaraṅkaḷ erintu cāmparāyiṉa; patiṉeṇṇāyiram makkaḷum maṭintupōyiṉar!
iṅṅaṉam, uyir vāḻkkaikkup pēriṭar payappatāṉa tīvaṭiviṉatāy mutaṟkaṇ iruntamaiyāl, innilavulakattiṉ mēṟparappiṟ palakōṭi āṇṭukaḷ varaiyiṟ puṟpūṇṭukaḷ mutal makkaḷ īṟāṉa ettakaiya uyirun tōṉṟāvāyiṉa. piṟaku, paṉṉūrāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka innilavulakiṉ vemmai āṟi varavara, itaṉ mēṟparappu iṟuka, iṟukiya kaṟpāṟaiyiṉ paḷḷattākkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil maḻainīr nirampi, ēri, ciṟukaṭal peruṅkaṭal mutalāṉa nīrnilaikaḷait tōṟṟuvikkalāyiṟṟu.
aṅṅaṉam uṇṭāṉa nīrnilaikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil nīrppūṇṭukaḷum, avaṟṟiṉpiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittup, perukkak kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟi nam kaṭpulaṉkaḷukkuc ciṟitum pulaṉākāta mika nuṇṇiya uyirkaḷun tōṉṟuva vāyiṉa. avaṟṟiṉpiṟ pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu naṅ kaṭpulaṉkaḷukkup pulaṉākum īraṟivuyirkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ mūvaṟivu, nālaṟivu aiyaṟivuṭaiya uyirkaḷum, iṟutiyāka āṟaṟivuyirkaḷākiya makkaḷun tōṉṟalāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam nīriṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya uyirkaḷiṟ pala nilattiṉmēl ivarntu vantu uyirvāḻtaṟku ēṟṟapaṭiyāka, nīr nilaikaḷai aṭutta nilappakutikaḷiṟ ciṟiyavum periyavumāṉa puṟpūṇṭukaḷum, marañceṭi koṭikaḷum uṇṭāki ilaiyum pūvaṅ, kāyuṅ kaṉiyuṅ, kiḻaṅkukaḷum vittukkaḷum ākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷai mikutiyāka aḷittaṉa. uṇavup poruḷkaḷ mikuntu varavē, avaṟṟai ayilum uyirkaḷum oṉṟu, pattu, nūṟu, āyiram, patiṉāyiram, nūṟāyiramāyp palkalāyiṉa.
ivvāṟāka, nilattiṉ mēṟparappil nīrttōṟm, kaṟpoṭittōṟṟam, puṟpūṇṭuttōṟṟam, ciṟṟuyirttōṟṟam mutalāṉa nikaḻccikaḷellām paṭippaṭiyē yuṇṭākip, palakōṭi āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟa piṉṉarētāṉ makkaḷuyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam uḷatāyiṟṟu. vilaṅkiṉ yākkaiyil niṉṟa uyir palakōṭi yāṇṭukaḷāka avviyākkaikaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭuk kaṭaippaṭiyāka makkaḷ yākkaiyil varalāṉatu iṟṟaikku ainnūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyaṉṟi, ataṟku muṉ makkaḷuṭampuṭaiya ettakaiya uyirumē illaiyeṉṟu ittuṟaiyiṟ pēruḻaippeṭuttup peritārāynta nila nūl uyir nūlvalla mēlnāṭṭāciriyarkaḷ muṭivukaṭṭi yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ākavē, im maṇmicait tōṉṟiya uyirttokaikaḷiṟ, kaṭaimuṟaiyākat tōṉṟiyavai makkaḷuṭampu vāynta āṟaṟivuyir kaḷēyeṉṟum, ivai tōṉṟiya kālam ainnūṟāyira āṇṭukaḷukkumēṟ ceṉṟatākāteṉṟum niṉaiviṟ patittal vēṇṭum.
2. makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya iṭam
iṉi, innilavulakiṉ entap pakutiyil mutaṉ mutal ellā uyirkaḷum vāḻat tuvaṅkiṉa eṉpatu ārāyaṟpāṟṟu. nīraiviṭṭu nilattiṉmēṟ pōnta piṉ uyirkaḷ paciyāl vāṭi maṭiyāmal nilaippaṭutaṟku vēṇṭiyavai uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷ allavō? ākavē, uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaṉmutal enta iṭattil mikutiyāyt tōṉṟik kiḷarntaṉavō, anta iṭamē uyirkaḷ naṉku uyirvāḻntu palkutaṟku ēṟṟa peruvaḷaṉ uṭaiyatātal vēṇṭumeṉpatai evarumē aṟivar. innilavulakiṉ vaṭamuṉaip pāṅkarum, itaṉ teṉmuṉaip pāṅkarum aṉṟum iṉṟum paṉippāṟaikaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, aṅkē puṟpūṇṭukaḷum marañceṭi koṭikaḷum uṇṭāvatillai. avai yillaiyākavē uyirvāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyā uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷum appakutikaḷiṟ kiṭaippatillai. ākavē, vaṭamuṉai teṉmuṉaikaḷai neruṅkiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟi vāḻntiruttal iyalātu.
piṉṉar, av vaṭamuṉai teṉmuṉaikaḷukku mika vilaki, innilattiṟku naṭuvaṇatāṉa kumari nāṭē, pakal irāppoḻutukaḷum veppataṭpaṅkaḷum otta nilaiyil iyaṟkaiyē poruntappeṟṟu, marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṭarntu vaḷarntu, nel, kōtumai, palvakaip payaṟukaḷ, tīñcuvaik kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutalāṉa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷai vaḻaṅkutaliṉ, ītoṉṟē makkaḷum piṟa uyirkaḷum mutaṉmutaṟṟōṉṟi uyirvāḻntu, palkip palamukamāyp paravutaṟku iṭañceytateṉpatu āḻnta ārāycciyāl naṉku viḷaṅkā niṟkiṉṟatu.
ivviyalpiṉatāṉa kumarināṭē makkaṭṭōṟṟattiṟku mutaṟṟāyakamāy irunta uṇmaiyiṉai mēṉāṭṭā ciriyarkaḷ palamukamāyp parakka ārāyantu muṭipu kaṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	naṉimikap paḻaṅkālattu makkaḷ mutaṉmutal tōṉṟi vāḻnta iṭam teṉṟamiḻk kumarināṭē eṉa valiyuṟuttappaṭuvatāl tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻ makkaḷum, tamiḻar nākarikamumē muṉṉuḷḷaṉa eṉpataṉai valiyuṟutti innūlai viritteḻuta aṭikaḷār toṭaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl ataṉaip piṉ iṇaippāka īṇṭuc cērttuḷḷām.

paṇṭaikkālat tamiḻarum āriyarum
- muṟṟum -


Preface to the Second Edition
This Tamil work on ‘Caste and Its Evil’ first appeared in the form of a somewhat long essay in the sixth volume of my Tamil magazine Jnanasagaram in October, 1911. The occasion which called it into existence was in this wise. In response to the invitation of the Saiva Siddhanta Sabha at Tuticorin, I had to go there in December, 1910, in order to preside at its grand annual gathering and so to conduct it as to make it meet the needs of the time. I took an active part in its proceedings, as it was ably managed by learned, intelligent and well-meaning Saivites and was also attended by a large number of men and women of enlightenment, earnestness and understanding. Everything went on smoothly and delightfully on the first day, except in one important respect. Able lecturers filled with enthusiasm for introducing reforms into religious and social matters, came from distant parts of the country, but some of them were not treated with that respect which learning, good intentions and good manners claim, but were treated, as appeared to my mind, rather badly, simply because they happened to belong to non-vegetarian castes. Whether learned or unlearned, good or bad, religious or irreligious only those who were known to be Saivites, that is, those only whose lineage was known to have originated with the vegetarian velala parents, were invited to sit in one hall and dine together, while others, whose adherence to Saiva religion and clean vegetarian mode of living could not be questioned but whose only fault was what was occasioned by the mere accident of birth, were made to sit aloof in a separate place and served meals rather lately. This I observed on the first day and, on the next, I questioned some of the prominent members of the society why they were treated thus. One amongst them replied me that they belonged to non-vegtetarain castes, and, therefore, could not be admitted into the dining - hall of the Saivites. But I said that though they were no born vegetarians, they too were Saivites in so far as they followed the principles of Saiva religion and adopted the vegetarian mode of living, and that, therefore, they ought to be encouraged, so that other like them might also come up in the hope of mingling with a higher class people and lead a purer and a truer form of religious life. I added that, if their object of conducting such religious meetings were sincere, they should spread the beneficial, teachings of the Saiva religion not in brotherly love and affection towards each other as all are the children of a single Heavenly Father Lord Siva and by prompting those of them who are morally and intellectually stronger to uplift the weaker and make them their own, and that such merciful, tender and benevolent kind of service by drawing together the choice people from every class and community, would not only tend to increase the strength of the Saivite community but would also heighten the value of their service to humanity at large. To this the man curtly replied that it was not the custom to do so. Again, I argued with him at some length pointing out to him that what he called ‘the custom’ was not really so but the reverse of it was the true one as is evident in the lives of the Saints and Sages who founded and spread the Saiva religion. But unfortunately the man was not amenable to reason, since his false and arrongant notion of the superiority of his caste blinded his whole mental vision. Thereupon I made up my mind to dine rather with the excluded party than with such self - conceited Saivites. And accordingly I did dine with the forbidden brethren. But so much did this action of mine enrage the Saivite brethren that, on the next day, they forbade me entering their dining hall during meal-times. This put me on my mettle and I told them point blank that, as the way to my room lay through their dining - hall, I could not go but through it as usual; and said also that I should not take the meals cooked by their men under their supervision, since i thought the meals prepared in the midst of such inhuman people get themselves contaminated. On that day, the sitting of the Conference was to commence at 3 p.m. with me in the chair; it was nearly 2 p.m., still I had not touched my meals; having seen me persevering in my determination, all the Saivite members, except one or two, had the kindness to come to me and apologize for what the had done and expressed their willingness to treat henceforth the lecturers and other with equality and without minding any caste - distinctions. Of course, there were one or two dissenting elements but these were eliminated from our group. We then sat together, prayed to God for blessing us with that bond of unity, and partook of our meals with one joyous heart. On that third sitting day of the conference, the lectures had been very illuminating and the proceedings were brought to a successful close by my concluding speech which impressed on the minds of the audience the urgent necessity of cutting at the root the evils of caste - distinctions.
Nevertheless, a few mischievous elements with whom caste was everything, while virtue, learning, intelligence. piety, religiousness and such other great qualities count for nothing, could not be silenced either by reasoning or by any regard to social unity, but they bestirred themselves most actively and attacked me in a magazine - article which, with much abusive matter, asserted the superiority of their caste on false and most erroneous grounds. I might have treated it with indifference, for it contained no argument worth contending for, but for the harmful influence it was likely to have on the minds of the unenlightened. I had, therefore, to take up the cudgels on behalf of the down - trodden and in the interest also of the true Saiva community whose progress consisted not in its exclusiveness but in absorbing into it and assimilating with itself persons of higher qualities turned out from lower orders and having an eager expectation to be lifted up from above; and the result was the  first edition of this treatise.
The first edition was merely a booklet of 22 pages, which dealt chiefly with the practical aspect of the caste question, while its theoretical part it touched but rather too briefly. How the barriers raised between one caste and another are mere fictitious ones existing only in the imagination of certain class of people in whom it was bred by their own arrogance and ignorance, how a mixture of castes results as a product of natural human needs and takes place both openly and covertly from the very beginning of the human family, and how easily a fusion of the good and cultured people picked up from every caste and community can be effected for the immense benefit of all, were clearly shown in it. That the tenets of Saiva religion do not stand against but lend a free, unstinted, undisguised support to such a whole-some fusion of the select, was also shown by quotations taken direct from the writings of the founders and exponents of tha religion. How useful the booklet proved to those who looked forward with eagerness to such a reform of castes, thought they themselves had not the courage to express the need of it might be seen from the rapid sale the thousan copies of it had in a few months.
Inevitably the book had been long out of print, though demands for it increased day after day. The elaborte research - work unaided I had to do, in order to redeem the literary and religious history of the land from the thick gloom of myth and uncertainty created and cast over it by selfish and selfinterested persons, the constantanduremitting study I pursued of works on ‘New psychology’, stimulated by a strong desire to introduce its subjects into Tamil, left me little leisure to devote to the preparation of its second edition earlier. But, now that I was enabled to undertake the work, years of study and research - work facilitating it for me, I set myself to enlarging the briefly - touched - subject on ‘The origin and growth of the institution of caste,’ since I thought that, for healing its present corruption and infusing new life into it, only a thorough knowledge of its past history could afford a proper and effective remedy. And accordingly the subject has been amplified and treated fully and extensively in the first five chapters of this edition.
In the first chapter, it is shown that the present day castes cannot be identified with those of the past since the callings of the former have undergone so radical a change as is opposed to the ordinances laid down in the Sanscrit Vedas and Dharma Sastras. Modern castes have become hardened into unworkable masses by losing completely their pliancy of meaning and original good quality. When the composition of the first nine mandalas of the Rig Veda took place, the four castes were quite unknown to the Aryans. But, by the time when the tenth was added, the institution of four castes had become almost an established fact. Still it should be borne in mind that this division of the Indian people into four castes was based merely upon division of labour and was introduces as a convenient theoretic classification intended for the purposes of social and economical sciences. Practically there was no any hard and fast rule that one caste shouldnot adopt the profession of another nor should the one mix with another. Many Dravidian kings, merchants and even slaves took part in the composition of the Vedic hymns and followed professions that suited best their taste and capacity.
In the second chapter, the same subject is continued and discussed how, even at the time of the twelve oldest upanishads and the two epics the Mahabharata and the Ramayana when attempts were made by brahmins to make castes hereditary and exalt themselves at the expense of others, only merit and not birth was regarded as constituting a high caste.
In the third chapter are shown how, at the time when a series of Sanscrit works called Dharma Sastras came to be written, the hereditary claims of castes were just beginning to tighten their hold on the people, how the brahmins were attempting to demand exclusive privileges for themselves and devise the cruellest and the most oppressive measures for the labouring classes, how, even then, importance was attached not to birth but to merit, and how the brahmins and others who failed, in the smallest degree, to perform the prescribed duties and functions of their caste lost their caste and became low-born. Here, for the first time, the mischief is pointed out of including the Tamilians in the Sudra or slave caste. The institution of caste-system originated not with the Aryans as is erroneously supposed by oriental scholars, but with the statesmen of the ancient Tamilians themselves who had attained to a high degree of civilisation long before the Aryans entered India.
In the fourth chapter, a critical inquiry of the ‘Tolkappiam’, a very ancient Tamil work which is older then even some portions of the Rig-Veda, is taken up - especially of its sections dealing with the social classifications of the Tamil people. How the Tamil people reached the agricultural stage many centuries before when the Aryans were still in the pastoral stage leading the life of nomads and how the life of an agricultural people necessitated the classification of its men and women into different classes according to their occupations, are clearly proved by the aid of its study. Though this division of the Tamil people was rendered necessary by the conditions of their civilized life, there was no prohibition in any Tamil classics of old of any caste - intermixture. On the other hand there are clear references in them to free intermingling of castes.
In ther fifth chapter in which the later day Saiva and Vaishnava religious literatures are taken up for solving this intricate and difficult problem of caste, are given numerous instances and clear quotations from the lives and writings of the Saints of the two great religious, in proof that it was only Love towards God and his servants and not caste was regarded and respected in them.
In the sixth chapter the whole of the first edition which dealt with the practical aspect of the castequestion is included with necessary alterations.
In  the seventh and th concluding chapter, easy and practical means for wiping away caste-distinctions and consolidating the refined elements coming above from every grade of society, are set forth.
Another prominent feature of this edition is its pure Tamils style entirely free from Sanscrit words.
In fine it is my great pleasure to express my thanks and heart-felt gratitude to my esteemed friend Mr. C. Ariya Nayagam, Proctor, at Matale (Ceylon), for the extreme kindness with which he promptly sent me a charitable contribution of Rs 250 towards the printing expenses of the book. Words are inadequate to express sufficiently my deep debt of gratitude to this generous-hearted gentleman for similar contributions which he was making frequently for the past ten years and more towards the printing expenses of my writings. But for his munificent help, I would have been much embarassed in carrying out my literary and religious work successfully. By the grace of God may he live long in all glory!

The Sacred Order of Love	VEDACHALAM
Pallavaram,
1st June, 1926.

6.	ulaka vaḻakkilum cāti illai	136
7.	cāti vēṟṟumaikaḷai oḻikkum vaḻikaḷ	151

cāti vēṟṟumaiyum pōlic caivarum
cāttiram palapēcuñ caḻakkarkāḷ
kōttiramuṅ kulamuṅkoṇ ṭeṉceyvīr,
pāttirañ civameṉṟu paṇitirēl
māttiraikkuḷ ḷaruḷum māṟpēṟarē.
tirunāvukkaracu cuvāmikaḷ
iyal - 1
paḻaiya vētattil cāti illai
caivacamayamāṉatu aruḷ oḻukkattaiyum, civapirāṉ māṭṭum avaṉ aṭiyār māṭṭum ēṉaiyellā uyirkaḷ māṭṭum aṉpupūṇṭoḻukum oḻukkattaiyum aṟivuṟut tuvateṉṟu uṇarum nalviṉai illāta pōlic caivar cilar cātivēṟṟumaiyē tām caivacamayattāṟ peṟum meyyuṇarvākum eṉṟu mayaṅki uṇarntu, am mayakka vuṇarcciyait tammiṭattum tammō ṭottavariṭattum vaittu makiḻvatōṭu amaitipeṟāmal, ataṉait tamakku icainta pattirikaikaḷiṉum eḻuti veḷiyiṭuvittuc, caiva vuṇmaikaḷai uḷḷavāṟē eṭuttu viḷakkivarum tava voḻukkamuṭaiya periyāraiyum tamakkuḷḷa poṟāmai yālum iḻintataṉmaiyālum tamakkut tōṉṟiyavā ṟellām ikaḻntu pēcutaluñ ceykiṉṟār. oruvaruṭaiya uyarvun tāḻvum aṟivāṉ mikka cāṉṟōr matikkaṟ pālarē yallāmal, aṟivillāk kuṟumpar matikkaṟpāla rallar; ākavē, aṟivillār kūṟum ikaḻuraikaḷaiyum poym moḻikaḷaiyum aṟivuṭaiyār oru poruṭṭāka eṇṇārākaliṉ avar kūṟum paḻic coṟkaḷai viṭuttu, avar kūṟum maṟṟap pakutikaḷil uḷḷa kuṟṟaṅkaḷai maṭṭum oru ciṟitu eṭuttukkāṭṭi maṟuttal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu ārāycci yuṇarvillātār mayaṅkāmaip poruṭṭēyām.
